## Core Project Concepts

_Agricultural Transition_{: .glossary #agricultural-transition}
: The [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) was the *profound chronological shift* in [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) where humanity moved away from small, nomadic _hunter-gatherer bands_{: .neon} to permanent _farming_{: .bold}, large _civilizations_{: .bold}, industrialized _economies_{: .bold}, and the modern _digital present_{: .bold}, creating massive [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). For roughly **300,000 years** (and **millions of years** as earlier humans and primates), [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) *evolved to thrive* in intimate, [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging [groups]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) defined by *constant movement*, high [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy), and *deep interpersonal* [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), [co-regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation) and [reciprocity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reciprocity). The advent of agriculture roughly 12,000 years ago abruptly _ruptured_{: .warn} this *ancestral environment*. By settling into densely populated communities, humans triggered a cascade of _artificial_{: .warn} societal structures: the accumulation of private property, rigid social [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies), coercive institutions, and the need for a [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) to manage it all. Because biological [evolution]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) operates at *a glacial pace*, our brains and bodies are *still* _hardwired_{: .bold} for the nomadic band, leaving us struggling to navigate the massive, anonymous, and stressful systems of the modern world. Understanding this profound transition is the foundational _key to recognising_{: .cool} that the societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) and the [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), made-up [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) we consider [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) today are not biological inevitabilities, but recent, *artificial workarounds* that must be **rigorously questioned** from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

_(See ["Neolithic Revolution" on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neolithic_Revolution){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_Clean Slate_{: .glossary #clean-slate}
: Approaching a problem, [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), or societal norm _without preconceived notions_{: .neon}, [inherited biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}), or _assumed_{: .warn} traditions, relying instead on [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) and what can actually be proven. From an evolutionary perspective, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) _actively resists_{: .bold} a clean slate. To *conserve cognitive energy* and ensure *immediate tribal belonging*, we [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to instinctively absorb the [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and social [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) of our [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) without question. Today, however, this **default mechanism** leaves us *intellectually* _trapped_{: .warn}. We passively accept the deeply entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins), coercive institutions, and [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) of massive post-agricultural societies as if they were "undeniable" laws of nature simply because they are historically [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}). Achieving a clean slate is therefore an unnatural, deliberate act of cognitive rebellion. It requires *consciously* wiping away intersubjective myths, *actively suspending* our biological craving for [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty), and *ruthlessly applying* a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to rebuild our understanding of reality from the ground up, using only [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}).

**See also:** [The Clean Slate Challenge]({{ "/guide/clean-slate/" | relative_url }})

---

_Critical Thinking_{: .glossary #critical-thinking}
: The [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), *disciplined* _analysis_{:neon} and _evaluation_{: .neon} of an issue in order to form a _judgment_{: .bold}, requiring the *active questioning* of _assumptions_{: .warn} and _unevidenced_{: .warn} [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority). Our brains [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) for tribal cohesion and rapid [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) mental shortcuts known as "[heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics)". It **did not** evolve not for discovering [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth. [Critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) is the *conscious application* of the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) to **counter** our [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) biases and deconstruct the [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) realities of [post-Agricultural]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) societies.

_(See [Critical Thinking on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Critical_thinking){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_Evidence_{: .glossary #evidence}
: [Objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [verifiable facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}) and data used to _support_{: .neon} or _refute_{: .neon} a _claim_{: .bold}, _independent_{: .cool} of *personal feelings*, [cultural narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) *interpretations*. Evolutionarily, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) was not "designed" to prioritize [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}). In our *ancestral environment*, [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) depended heavily on *maintaining* [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group); *accepting* [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) shared myths guaranteed social inclusion, while demanding physical proof over loyalty risked deadly _ostracisation_{: .warn}. Today, this *biological blind spot* is a profound [mismatch]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mismatches). It makes us highly susceptible to [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas), [moral panics]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/moral-panics/" | relative_url }}), and the unquestioned [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) enforced by a modern [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood). Because we naturally *confuse* [correlation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#correlation) with causation and *mistake* strong emotions or [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty) for truth, we easily fall for manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) rather than demanding hard data. Within a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), evidence is the absolute, non-negotiable currency of reality. It is *the only reliable tool we have* to bypass our [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}). By demanding [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) observation and strict [testability]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#testability), we cut through intersubjective illusions and anchor our understanding to [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), ensuring our beliefs are based on what is demonstrably true rather than what is merely socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}).

_(See [Scientific Evidence on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scientific_evidence){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_Evolution_{: .glossary #evolution}
: The _gradual process_{: .neon} by which living organisms *change and diversify over generations* through genetic _mutations_{: .bold}, [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}), and adaptation to changing environments. Crucially for this project, evolution is **not just** the *origin of our physical bodies*, but the *absolute architect of our* [psychology]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), social behaviours, and cognitive defaults. For roughly **300,000 years** as [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) and **millions of years** as earlier humans, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) was meticulously calibrated for [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) in small, intimate hunter-gatherer bands. However, biological evolution *operates at a glacial pace*, while cultural and technological changes *accelerate exponentially*. Because our [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) has *barely changed* since the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), we are now forced to navigate massive, anonymous, and highly complex modern civilizations using brains _hardwired for a fundamentally different world_{: .mark}. This extreme speed differential is the root cause of profound [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). By treating evolutionary biology as a [first principle]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can **stop moralizing human flaws**—such as [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) [tribalism]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}), our craving for [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty), or our vulnerability to emotional [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing)—and start viewing them objectively as [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) mechanisms taken out of context. A [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) uses this understanding to deconstruct the artificial [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) that _exploit_{: .warn} our ancient _instincts_{: .bold}, providing the necessary foundation for true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation).

_(See [Evolution on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Evolution){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_First Principles_{: .glossary #first-principles}
: The _fundamental, foundational axioms_{: .bold} that cannot be deduced any further; starting from a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) to build an understanding of reality based only on what is demonstrably and [objectively]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) true. Evolutionarily, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) actively avoids first-principles thinking because it is *intensely cognitively expensive*. In our ancestral environment, [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) relied on *rapid* [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics), *gut instincts*, and unquestioningly adopting the shared [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) of the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). Pausing to deduce the underlying physics of a rustling bush from scratch would likely result in being eaten; _pure instinct kept you alive_{: .mark}. In the complex, post-[Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition) world, this biological shortcut leaves us incredibly vulnerable. Instead of analyzing reality critically, we _blindly accept_{: .warn} deeply entrenched societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins), manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing), and the unevidenced [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) handed down by authority figures and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood)—mistaking what is merely culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) for an immutable law of nature. Applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) demands the *radical, unnatural effort* of [first-principles thinking]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}). It requires _ruthlessly stripping away_{: .neon} all [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}), moral [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty), and intersubjective myths until you hit the solid bedrock of [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), allowing you to rebuild your conclusions step-by-step from **undeniable reality**.

_(See [First principle on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_principle){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_Human History_{: .glossary #human-history}
: The *vast chronological timeline* stretching back **millions of year**s, tracking the biological and cultural [evolution]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) of early human species and culminating in the roughly **300,000-year** story of modern humans ([Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens)). For *over 99.5% of this timeline*, our species survived and biologically adapted exclusively within _intimate, nomadic hunter-gatherer bands_{: .mark}. During this deep ancestral period, our brains hardwired the psychological traits necessary for immediate [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival): intense [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) loyalty, [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), high personal [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy), and a reliance on shared myths. However, the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition) roughly 12,000 years ago _radically ruptured_{: .warn} this environment. In an evolutionary blink of an eye, humanity scaled into massive, anonymous, and sedentary civilizations. Because biological evolution operates at a *glacial pace* and cannot keep up with rapid cultural change, much of recorded human history is actually the story of profound [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}): ancient brains desperately trying to navigate the artificial [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins), coercive [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies), and [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas) of the modern world. Without grasping the true, overwhelming scale of deep history, we suffer from _temporal myopia_{: .warn}—*blindly assuming* that our current societal structures are [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) simply because they are what we consider normal today. By examining deep history through a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), we achieve a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), recognizing that much of modern [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) is a very recent, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) invention that can—and often should—be _actively questioned_{: .neon} from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

---

_In-Group and Out-Group_{: .glossary #in-group-and-out-group}
: The deeply [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) *psychological tendency* to divide the world into **"us"** (the [in-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}), who we *instinctively favour, trust, and protect*) and **"them"** (the out-group, who we *view with suspicion*, **judge harshly**, or _dehumanize_{: .warn}). For hundreds of thousands of years, this tribal dualism was a highly effective [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) strategy. In a dangerous ancestral environment defined by scarce resources, maintaining absolute loyalty and [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) within your small nomadic band was essential, while **trusting strangers often meant death**. However, in the massive, interconnected civilizations following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), this biological default has become a _catastrophic_{: .warn} [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Because our brains still desperately crave a tribe to survive, we now project this ancient "us vs. them" instinct onto abstract, artificial categories—political parties, nations, religions, "race", immigration and [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas). This _psychological hijacking_{: .warn} causes individuals to fiercely defend irrational [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) and destructive [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) simply to signal loyalty to their team, often [weaponising]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) the in-group's [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear), moral [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty), and [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) against the out-group. Overcoming this [instinct] is the ultimate test of a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview). It requires the *unnatural, conscious effort* to separate an idea from tribal identity, demanding that all claims be evaluated purely on [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), _regardless of whose "team" proposed them_{: .mark}.

_(See [In-group and out-group on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/In-group_and_out-group){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_Lock-Ins_{: .glossary #lock-ins}
: _Deeply ingrained_{: .bold} societal habits, laws, or ways of [thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) that have become so established and normalized over time that they are *incredibly difficult to change* or even question, regardless of their actual usefulness or the harm they cause. From an evolutionary perspective, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) is biologically predisposed to accept lock-ins because it is a _cognitive miser_{: .neon}; questioning every established routine requires **immense metabolic energy**, whereas blindly following the [in-group's]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) traditions is *mentally cheap* and ensures social safety. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), these simple tribal habits scaled into _massive, rigid structural systems_{: .mark}—such as coercive educational [curricula]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#curricula), rigid economic paradigms, and bureaucratic institutions. Because we suffer from _temporal myopia_{: .warn}, we look at these relatively recent, artificial systems and mistakenly assume they are immutable laws of nature simply because they are historically [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}). These lock-ins are fiercely protected by [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing), and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood), who [weaponise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) the [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of chaos to prevent systemic change. Escaping a lock-in requires true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). It demands the conscious application of a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and _ruthlessly deconstruct_{: .neon} the system down to its [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), replacing historical momentum with [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Mismatches_{: .glossary #mismatches}
: **(Also known as** [Evolutionary Mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }})**).** Situations where human traits, instincts, or behaviours that [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to be _vital_{: .cool} for [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) in our ancient ancestral environment are no longer suitable—and are frequently _actively harmful_{: .warn}—in our modern, densely populated, and highly technological society. For hundreds of thousands of years, the human body and [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) were *meticulously calibrated* by [natural selection]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural-selection) for a very specific reality: small, nomadic hunter-gatherer bands defined by *constant physical movement*, face-to-face [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), and immediate existential threats. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), cultural and technological innovation **accelerated exponentially**, radically altering our physical and social environments while our [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) _remained virtually unchanged_{: .mark}. This profound lag means we are constantly trying to navigate massive modern complexities with _Paleolithic software_{: .terminal}. Our [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) craving for calorie-dense foods, once essential for surviving famine, now drives modern health crises. Similarly, our hardwired [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of social ostracism, once necessary to stay safely within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group), is now [weaponised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) _by algorithms_{: .warn}, [coercive]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) institutions, and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) to enforce compliance to artificial [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}). Recognizing these mismatches is the foundational step toward true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), we can _stop moralizing these biological misfires_{: .mark} and start objectively diagnosing them. This allows us to return to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and actively redesign our societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), ensuring they support our actual [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing) rather than exploiting our ancient vulnerabilities.

_(See [Evolutionary Mismatch on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Evolutionary_mismatch){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_Narratives_{: .glossary #narratives}
: The [stories]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), myths, or intersubjective [belief systems]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) that societies invent and pass down to make sense of the world, organize populations, and justify [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) or [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). From an evolutionary perspective, narratives are the _ultimate cognitive hack_{: .neon} that allowed [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) to dominate the planet. Because we can only maintain genuine personal trust with a small number of individuals, our species [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) the unique ability to *cooperate flexibly in the millions* by believing in shared fictions. To the ancestral [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}), accepting [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) narrative was the *price of admission* to the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group); rejecting it meant **social death and ostracism**. However, in the complex, post-[Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition) world, this biological dependency has become a _profound vulnerability_{: .warn}. Today, massive societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) and coercive institutions are maintained by a modern [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) who actively [weaponise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) emotional [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to _manufacture consent_{: .bold} for their [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas). Because our brains *naturally conflate* a compelling story with [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth, these narratives often persist as [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) long after they are proven false, exploiting our biological craving for [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty). _Breaking free_{: .cool} from inherited narratives requires true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). It demands the conscious, unnatural application of a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and relentlessly subject every cultural [myth]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#myth) to the _harsh light_{: .neon} of [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Natural vs. Normal_{: .glossary #natural-vs-normal}
: A *vital distinction*: "[Natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }})" refers to traits or behaviours that [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) biologically over deep [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) time; "Normal" simply means whatever a specific [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) has currently chosen to [standardize]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation) or accept as common practice. For hundreds of thousands of years, there was no distinction between the two. What was culturally normal for an ancestral hunter-gatherer band—*constant physical movement*, egalitarian({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), and high [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy)—was perfectly aligned with what was biologically [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), human cultures began inventing _massive, artificial societal structures_{: .mark} at a speed biological evolution could not match. Today, this creates profound [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Much of what we consider "normal" in modern life—sedentary indoor jobs, rigid educational [curricula]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#curricula), and [isolated nuclear families]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }})—is _fundamentally unnatural_{: .warn} to our physiology and [psychology]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}). Because the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) suffers from *temporal myopia*, we instinctively conflate the two, _blindly accepting_{: .warn} deeply entrenched societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) as immutable laws of nature simply because everyone around us accepts them. This confusion is *actively exploited* by the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood), who use [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) to convince us that their artificial systems are _biological inevitabilities_{: .bold}. The core discipline of a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) is _actively severing this false equivalency_{: .mark}. By returning to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and evaluating our environment from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can _stop pathologizing humans_{: .cool} for failing to adapt to unnatural systems, and instead use [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) to redesign what is "normal" so that it _actually supports_{: .cool} [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) [human flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing).

---

_Scientific Method_{: .glossary #scientific-method}
: A _systematic, self-correcting process_{: .cool} of acquiring knowledge that involves making observations, forming a [hypothesis]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hypothesis), [testing]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) it through rigorous [experimentation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#experiment), and adjusting conclusions based strictly on [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}). Evolutionarily, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) _actively resists_{: .bold} the scientific method. In our ancestral environment, [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) favoured *rapid* [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics), *emotional intuition*, and unquestioning belief in the [in-group's]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) shared myths. We are _biologically hardwired_{: .bold} to seek immediate [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty) and confirm our existing [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) to maintain social belonging; actively trying to prove ourselves wrong is a _profoundly unnatural, metabolically expensive act_{: .mark}. Today, this biological aversion to self-correction leaves us _incredibly vulnerable_{: .warn}. When we abandon the scientific method, we default to tribal thinking, allowing [coercive]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) institutions and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) to use [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) and emotional [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to maintain societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins). Without it, we easily mistake unevidenced [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) traditions for [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth. The scientific method is the *operational engine* of a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) and the _ultimate tool_{: .cool} for intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By demanding strict [testability]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#testability) and forcing our assumptions to collide with physical reality, it _ruthlessly strips away_{: .neon} [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas), allowing us to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and rebuild our understanding from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

_(See [Scientific Method on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scientific_method){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_Scientific Worldview_{: .glossary #scientific-worldview}
: A specific [worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#worldview) that _rejects_{: .bold} [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) and unevidenced [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), choosing instead to interpret reality based strictly on the _self-correcting application_{: .cool} of [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}), [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), and the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Secular Priesthood_{: .glossary #secular-priesthood}
: Institutional bureaucrats, experts, or [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) figures who do not rely on [religion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion) but still enforce [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), _unscientific compliance_{: .warn} and state [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) under the guise of technical expertise, morality, or benevolence. From an evolutionary perspective, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) is *hardwired to defer* to authority figures. In our ancestral environment, trusting [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) elders or shamans ensured social cohesion and kept the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) unified; challenging them was a dangerous gamble that risked _deadly ostracization_{: .warn}. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition) and the rise of massive, complex societies, this biological vulnerability became a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Today, the ancient role of the shaman has been replaced by a sprawling class of technocrats, administrators, and [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) managers. While they may use the aesthetic language of modern society, they function exactly like [traditional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#tradition) clerics: they protect deeply entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins), demand _unquestioning submission_{: .warn} to their [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas), and [weaponise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) moral [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) and manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) against anyone who dissents. They routinely masquerade intersubjective opinions as [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}), turning what is merely socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) into untouchable [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma). Breaking the influence of the secular priesthood is the _vital core act_{: .cool} of intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). It requires applying a ruthless [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), stripping away their _unearned authority_{: .bold} and demanding that every mandate they impose be justified by strict [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

*See Also:* [The Page "Secular Priesthood"]({{ "/guide/obstacles/secular-priesthood/" | relative_url }})

---

_Testability_{: .glossary #testability}
: The ability of a claim or [hypothesis]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hypothesis) to be investigated and *potentially proven wrong* through observation or [experiment]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#experiment); if a [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) cannot be [tested]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}), it cannot be scientifically validated. From an evolutionary perspective, the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) is _actively hostile_{: .warn} to testability. In our ancestral environment, [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) depended on unhesitating [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) and **absolute loyalty** to the [in-group's]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) shared myths. Demanding that a tribal belief be "proven wrong" was not seen as intellectual rigour; it was a _dangerous act of defiance_{: .mark} that risked deadly ostracization. Today, this biological blind spot is heavily exploited. Because our brains *naturally conflate* emotional [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty) with truth, we easily accept the unevidenced [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) enforced by the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood). These authority figures deliberately use moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to construct claims that are _fundamentally untestable_{: .warn}, shielding their [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas) behind unquestionable [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) rather than [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}). Within a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), testability (often known as falsifiability) is the _supreme, non-negotiable filter_{: .neon} for reality. It is the mechanism that separates what is merely socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) from what is actually true. By refusing to accept any premise that cannot be tested against [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), we achieve a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and ensure our understanding of the world is anchored firmly to undeniable [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

_(See [Testability on Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Testability){:target="_blank" rel="noopener"})._{: .subtle}

---

_True Believers_{: .glossary #true-believers}
: Individuals who have _entirely outsourced_{: .warn} their [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) to [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}). Driven by the deep [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) need for *tribal belonging*, they conflate [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) with [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) reality, _aggressively rejecting_{: .warn} [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) that threatens their [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) identity or the [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) of their [in-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }})

**See also:** [True Believers]({{ "/articles/philosophy/true-believers/" | relative_url }})

---

## General Glossary

_Abuse_{: .glossary #abuse}
: In modern discourse, a *highly emotionally charged* but _poorly defined_{: .warn} word. It frequently acts as an [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [placeholder]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#placeholder) for all the things [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) finds objectionable—from physical violence to untestable [religious]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion) indoctrination—without offering a clear, _objective path_{: .cool} to understanding or preventing the _systemic conditions that actually cause the harm_{: .mark}.

**See Also:** [Redefining Neglect and Abuse]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/neglect-and-abuse/" | relative_url }})

**See Also:** [Mistreatment]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mistreatment)

---

_Adaptation_{: .glossary #adaptation}
: The [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) or systemic process by which an organism, population, or [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture) develops traits and behavioral strategies that _enhance its ability_{: .cool} to survive and reproduce in a specific environment. In [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology), this is an *unguided, physical mechanism* driven by [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}); in [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), cultural adaptation allows societies to _rapidly adjust_{: .neon} to new challenges—like climate shifts or resource scarcity—without waiting for **slow genetic changes**.

---

_ADHD (Attention Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder)_{: .glossary #adhd-attention-deficit-hyperactivity-disorder}
: A modern _psychiatric label_{: .warn} applied to individuals whose [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) cognitive traits—such as *rapidly shifting attention*, high physical energy, and the need for immediate, stimulating feedback—clash with the sedentary, highly structured demands of modern institutions. From an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) perspective, these traits were likely _highly adaptive_{: .cool} in dynamic hunter-gatherer environments, making ADHD a prime example of an [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) _rather than an inherent biological deficit_{: .mark}.

---

_Adolescence_{: .glossary #adolescence}
: The developmental stage functioning as an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) mechanism for [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [risk-taking]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#risk-taking), driving individuals to _challenge established structures_{: .bold} and explore unknown environments. This distinct, highly adaptive [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) life stage, is designed for extreme _neural plasticity_{: .neon}, *skill acquisition*, and *social calibration*. In the ancestral environment, this period was characterized by a _gradual integration_{: .cool} into adult responsibilities; today, it is _heavily patholosized_{: .warn} as a period of inherent [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) to justify extending structural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) and *top-down institutional control*. (See [Storm and Stress]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#storm-and-stress))

**See also:** [Case Study: Adolescence]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/adolescence/" | relative_url }})

---

_Adult-Directed Activity_{: .glossary #adult-directed-activity}
: The *highly structured, schedule-heavy reality* of the modern post-industrial childhood. It represents a severe [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) that _actively suppresses_{: .warn} a child's hardwired need for _unstructured, peer-to-peer play_{: .cool}. By forcing [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) to constantly [conform]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) to adult timelines and [standardised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation) metrics, [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) stifles [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) neural plasticity and _replaces organic social learning with top-down compliance_{: .mark}.

---

_Affection and Pleasure_{: .glossary #affection-and-pleasure}
: [Evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) biological rewards that _reinforce pro-social behavior_{: .cool}, trust, and connection. Modern systems often view these as _suspicious or trivial_{: .warn}, prioritizing *endless economic output* and rigid behavioral [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) over [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) mammalian, primate and **human joy**.

---

_Agendas_{: .glossary #agendas}
: An [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) crusade or *moral mission* driven by the "best intentions" to _improve society_{: .cool}. Because agendas are rooted in [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) rather than the [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) application of the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}), they prioritize [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) *purity* over [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) reality. By _rejecting_{: .warn} [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) evidence, individuals pursuing an agenda frequently _create or enforce societal structures that cause far more harm and suffering_{: .mark} than the issues they originally intended to solve.

---

_Agency Detection_{: .glossary #agency-detection}
: An [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) psychological [bias]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) that causes humans to assume events are caused by *intentional agents* (like predators or [spirits]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agency-detection)). Highly useful for [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) in the wild, but frequently _misfires_{: .warn} in modern life by creating a foundation for [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) in _invisible or supernatural forces_{: .neon}.

---

_Age-Segregation_{: .glossary #age-segregation}
: The _highly unnatural_{: .warn}, modern practice of grouping humans (particularly [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) and adolescents) *strictly by birth year*. Throughout our [evolutionary history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), human [socialization]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/socialisation/" | relative_url }}) and learning occurred _organically_{: .cool} within *mixed-age tribal bands*, making [age-segregation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#age-segregation) a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) that frequently _stifles_{: .warn} [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) development and [co-regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation). By severing [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) and teenagers from the diverse, multi-generational [mentorship]({{ "/guide/human-history/mentorship/" | relative_url }}) of the ancestral [clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan), [age segregation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#age-segregation) *artificially amplifies* peer over-reliance, digital [echo chambers]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#echo-chambers), and the _toxic transmission_{: .warn} of [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [panic]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#panic).

---

_Aimlessness_{: .glossary #aimlessness}
: A _chronic_{: .warn} psychological state stemming from a severe [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). In the [ancestral clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan), purpose was *directly tied* to tangible, daily contributions to group [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) (hunting, defense, and [caregiving]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#alloparenting)). In the modern post-industrial economy, _abstract wage labour_{: .bold} and the systemic erosion of these direct roles leave many people _biologically deprived_{: .warn} of _meaningful contribution_{: .cool}, resulting in _societal distress and social withdrawal_{: .mark}.

---

_Algorithmic Fear_{: .glossary #algorithmic-fear}
: A _catastrophic_{: .warn} modern [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) where digital platforms are *explicitly engineered* to [exploit]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }})s hardwired [negativity bias]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}). By systematically prioritizing [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [outrage]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/moral-outrage/" | relative_url }}) and [panic]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#panic) over [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) reality, these systems capture human attention for _massive corporate profit_{: .bold} while _fundamentally paralyzing society's capacity_{: .mark} for [rational]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rational) analysis.

---

_Alienation_{: .glossary}
: The _profound psychological distress_{: .warn} and sense of disconnection that occurs when human beings are forced to navigate *rigid*, [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) systems that _clash fundamentally_{: .warn} with our baseline evolutionary [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology). Often a direct byproduct of the structural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), it arises from the friction between our [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate), hunter-gatherer need for _tight-knit community_{: .cool} and the _isolating, hierarchical realities_{: .mark} of complex modern civilizations.

---

_Alliance Formation_{: .glossary #alliance-formation}
: The [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) process of _building trust_{: .cool} and [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) within a group. Originally achieved through *shared play*, grooming, and [intimacy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation), it ensured mutual [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) in ancestral environments before being replaced by _formal contracts and laws_{: .bold}.

---

_Alloparenting_{: .glossary #alloparenting}
: A _collective_{: .cool} [parenting]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#intensive-parenting), caregiving or [mentoring]({{ "/guide/human-history/mentorship/" | relative_url }}) system where individuals other than the biological [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) (such as grandparents, siblings, or community members) _assist in caring for and raising children_{: .mark}—a practice *deeply rooted* in [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), especially the **millions of years** (of both early and [modern humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens)) before the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Altruism_{: .glossary #altruism}
: The [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate), _hardwired human instinct to help others_{: .mark}, share resources, and ensure _fairness_{: .cool} *without direct, immediate reward*. Evolutionarily selected to help tight-knit bands _thrive_{: .cool}, it is a biological reality that flourishes in cooperative systems but is often [suppressed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression) by *modern competitive* [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). (See [Altruism and Cruelty]({{ "/guide/human-history/altruism-and-cruelty/" | relative_url }}))

**See also:** [Altruism]({{ "/articles/science/altruism/" | relative_url }})

---

_Anchoring_{: .glossary #anchoring}
: A [cognitive bias]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) where the [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) *relies heavily* on the first piece of information it receives (the "anchor") when making subsequent judgments. In the *fast-paced, artificial environment* of modern [capitalism]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#capitalism) and [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media), this _hardwired mental shortcut_{: .bold} is frequently _weaponised_{: .warn} by institutions to set [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) expectations, _manipulate_{: .warn} consumer anxiety, and _frame debates_{: .neon} before [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) can even begin.

---

_Anecdotal Evidence_{: .glossary #anecdotal-evidence}
: Information based on *personal accounts*, [stories]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or *isolated incidents* rather than rigorous scientific study; *highly compelling* to the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}), but _notoriously unreliable for drawing broad conclusions_{: .mark}.

---

_Anonymity_{: .glossary #anonymity}
: The structural state achieved when a population *exceeds* natural [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) group sizes, resulting in the _breakdown_{: .warn} of *reputation-based trust* and necessitating new social [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). When surrounded by thousands of strangers without forming meaningful, [reciprocal]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reciprocity) social bonds, this represents a _severe_{: .warn} [mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) with our [evolutionary history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), where human brains were _hardwired to thrive within small, highly recognizable, and emotionally interconnected tribal communities_{: .mark}.

**See the case study:** [Community & Anonymity]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/community-and-anonymity/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Anthropology_{: .glossary #anthropology}
: The *scientific study* of [human biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology), [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), culture, and societal development across time. Within this project, _anthropology_{: .neon} is the foundational, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) lens required to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). It provides the strictly [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) *baseline of our species*, demonstrating that for over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), **Homo sapiens** and earlier humans _thrived_{: .cool} in [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian), _highly active, and intimately connected foraging bands_{: .mark}. By studying the [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) mechanics of [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan), evolutionary anthropology allows us to definitively map the _devastating_{: .warn} [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) triggered by the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}). It is the _antidote_{: .neon} to the culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) _engineered_{: .warn} by the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood). Without this evolutionary grounding, [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) assumes modern [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }})—such as *isolated nuclear families*, extreme [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies), and _chronic stress_{: .warn}—are simply "human nature". By applying anthropology from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can *strip away* these [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) fictions and recognise exactly what the human animal actually requires for genuine [flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing).

---

_Apartheid and Racial Segregation_{: .glossary #apartheid-and-racial-segregation}
: _Institutionalized regimes of systemic, legally enforced racial discrimination_{: .mark} and [oppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#oppression). These systems highlight how the law is *frequently* [weaponised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) to manufacture and protect _unevidenced_{: .bold} social [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) and *tribal in-groups*.

---

_Architecture of Isolation (Little Boxes)_{: .glossary #architecture-of-isolation}
: The physical manifestation of our profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}), characterized by the *mass construction* of detached, single-family homes that _trap a highly social, tribal species inside disconnected drywall terrariums_{: .mark}. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), our built environments were _organically designed_{: .cool} to support [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan). Camps and early villages maximized shared space; hearths were communal, [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) relied on *shared infrastructure*, and children roamed freely under the collective safety of the group's [shared eyes]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shared-eyes). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) and accelerating rapidly during the post-industrial suburban boom, this dense, interconnected architecture was intentionally replaced by sprawling grids of _atomized units_{: .warn} separated by fences and driveways. This artificial environment actively prevents daily, casual human connection, mandates **catastrophic ecological waste**, and functions as the _ultimate consumer trap_{: .warn}. Yet, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) successfully [frames]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) this isolation as the *pinnacle of societal success*—the culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) property dream. When politicians and media debate the modern "housing crisis," they merely argue for building *more* of these unnatural cages to treat the [symptoms]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/treating-symptoms/" | relative_url }}) of population growth, entirely ignoring the *fundamental design flaw*. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires wiping the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and viewing our habitats from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}). By doing so, we can recognise that [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) human [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing) demands dismantling these physical [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) and redesigning our built environment to _restore the natural infrastructure_{: .cool} of communal living.

*See Also:* [Little boxes]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/housing-crisis/" | relative_url }})

---

_Aristotelian_{: .glossary #aristotelian}
: The _epistemological trap_{: .warn} of substituting the unevidenced [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) of a "great thinker" for actual [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) investigation. For centuries, the *unquestioning reverence* for Aristotelian [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) effectively _froze scientific progress_{: .warn} in the West, perfectly illustrating how the human [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) desire to defer to a high-status leader (the "master") creates a structural [lock-in]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) that fiercely resists the _self-correcting engine_{: .cool} of the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Artificial Light_{: .glossary #artificial-light}
: A *ubiquitous feature* of the modern constructed environment that _overrides_{: .warn} the [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) solar cycles that guided [human biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) for millions of years. Exposure to artificial light—particularly in sedentary, industrialized workspaces—_chronically disrupts_{: .warn} [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) circadian rhythms, contributing heavily to sleep deprivation, **hormonal imbalances**, and systemic [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}).

---

_ASBOs_{: .glossary #asbos}
: Civil orders (now replaced by "Criminal Behaviour Orders") used in the UK to restrict the [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) of individuals. They represent a form of modern _legal control_{: .warn} that frequently _pathologizes_{: .warn} [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [adolescent]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/adolescence/" | relative_url }}) behaviour, nonconformity, or [poverty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#poverty) instead of addressing underlying [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Assumptions_{: .glossary #assumptions}
: Ideas, [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), or premises that are *accepted as true or certain* to be correct without any [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) proof or [empirical evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) to verify them.

---

_Atomised Existence_{: .glossary #atomised-existence}
: The *hyper-individualized*, fragmented state of modern life where human beings are _systematically stripped_{: .warn} of their [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), kin-based support networks. This isolation is a direct byproduct of [post-Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) societies that prioritize *economic utility* and [nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) models over our biological need for _deep community integration_{: .cool}.

---

_Attention Economy_{: .glossary #attention-economy}
: A modern economic reality where human attention is treated as a *scarce and valuable commodity*, prompting digital platforms to engineer algorithms that _maximize engagement_{: .warn}, often by stoking [outrage]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clicks-and-outrage) or controversy.

**See also:** [Attention Economy]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/attention/" | relative_url }})

---

_Authority_{: .glossary #authority}
: A person, institution, or text wielded as an *official source* of [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}), knowledge, or truth. In a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), authority holds _no inherent weight_{: .bold}; a claim must be accepted based on the strength of its [verifiable evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), not because an influential figure, ancient text, or institutional [hierarchy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) declares it to be true.

---

_Autism (Autism Spectrum Disorder)_{: .glossary #autism-autism-spectrum-disorder}
: A _neurodevelopmental variation_{: .cool} characterized by distinct differences in social communication, sensory processing, and pattern recognition. While frequently _medicalised_{: .warn} as a [disorder]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#disorder) in the highly stimulating, hyper-social, and [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) modern world, many autistic traits—such as *deep hyper-focus*, [systematic thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}), and resistance to unevidenced [social conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity)—likely provided significant [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) value and _specialized utility_{: .cool} in early human, kin-based environments.

---

_Autonomy_{: .glossary #autonomy}
: The profound biological need and *capacity to govern oneself*; [freedom of thought]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}), will, and action. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) and earlier humans operated in *fiercely* [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands where high personal autonomy was inextricably linked to collective [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). We [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) an intense, _hardwired drive_{: .bold} to _direct our own labour_{: .cool}, play, and decision-making *without coercive oversight*. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), the rise of massive, stratified societies required the _systemic_{: .warn} [suppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression) of this [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) instinct to *organize compliant populations*. Today, the _chronic denial_{: .warn} of autonomy in our modern workplaces and educational institutions is a severe [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}), triggering deep physiological stress and societal [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction). To protect these entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins), the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) actively _pathologises_{: .warn} our innate desire for self-governance, using manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to label it as defiance, laziness, or a dangerous rejection of [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) i.e many are we accused: "Do you have a problem with authority?" as if it were some kind of "failing". They _demand submission_{: .bold} to [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), convincing us that a highly managed, culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) life is inevitable. Achieving true [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining human needs from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can stop moralizing artificial compliance and recognise that _genuine autonomy is not a granted political privilege_{: .mark}, but an [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) prerequisite for _biological flourishing_{: .cool}.

**See Also:** ["Legal Custody"]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#legal-custody-dependency)

---

_Availability Bias_{: .glossary #availability-bias}
: An [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) heuristic where the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) judges the likelihood of an event based on *how easily a vivid, dramatic example comes to mind*. While highly effective in ancestral environments for remembering immediate physical threats (like a predator attack), it is _ruthlessly_{: .bold} [exploited]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) in modern societies by the [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media) and the attention economy to _manufacture_{: .warn} [moral panics]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/moral-panics/" | relative_url }}), **ignoring** [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) statistics in favour of *emotionally charged* [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Bad News_{: .glossary #bad-news}
: The modern [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media)s _deliberate_{: .warn} [exploitation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) of the human brain's [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) negativity [bias]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}). In the ancestral environment, paying immediate attention to negative stimuli (like predators or tribal conflict) was *vital for physical* [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). Today, institutional media pipelines [weaponise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) this biological instinct by _flooding_{: .bold} our cognition with endless [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) crises, *capturing our attention for profit* while _entirely obscuring_{: .warn} [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) human progress.

**See also:** [Bad News]({{ "/guide/obstacles/bad-news/" | relative_url }})

---

_Behaviour Modification_{: .glossary #behaviour-modification}
: Psychological techniques and institutional practices aimed at *altering human actions*, typically through *systems of reward, punishment, and conditioning*. While frequently presented as therapeutic or educational, these methods are often used by schools, prisons, and the state to _enforce_{: .warn} [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience) and [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) to [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}). They inherently _treat humans as blank slates to be programmed_{: .mark}, rather than addressing the underlying [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) or *systemic failures* driving the [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Behind Closed Doors_{: .glossary #behind-closed-doors}
: The *unnatural*, _isolated_{: .warn} state of the modern [nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) and private household following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), but especially the last 200 years and rising exponentially following WWII. Because of this *structural isolation*, _modern society has absolutely no idea what an individual might have been through or suffered in private_{: .mark}—a dangerous _vulnerability_{: .bold} and **systemic blind spot** completely alien to 99.9% of our [evolutionary human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Beliefs_{: .glossary #beliefs}
The internal acceptance, conviction, or psychological stance that a specific proposition, [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or [worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#worldview) is *"true"*. In [human psychology]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) can *exist entirely* _independent_{: .warn} of [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) reality; they may be _inherited_{: .bold} through cultural [socialisation]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/socialisation/" | relative_url }}), driven by *emotional needs*, or constructed from *unevidenced* [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), *making them* fundamentally _distinct from verified facts_{: .mark}.

---

_Best Intentions_{: .glossary #best-intentions}
: [Subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), *emotionally driven* _motivations_{: .bold} that frequently _fail_{: .warn} to produce [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [harm reduction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#harm-reduction). In complex, post-Agricultural societies, individuals and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/guide/obstacles/fear-mongering/" | relative_url }})({{ "/guide/obstacles/secular-priesthood/" | relative_url }}) often rely on their [best intentions]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas) to *justify* enforcing _unevidenced_{: .warn} [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and structural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}), *ignoring the systemic* [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) and _measurable harm their actions actually cause_{: .mark} (i.e the transportation of [kids]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) to Australia, forced adoptions, etc).

---

_Biases_{: .glossary #biases}
: Systematic, unconscious _errors_{: .warn} in human thinking that *warp how we perceive reality*, process information, and make decisions, often inherited as [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) _mental shortcuts_{: .bold}.

**See also:** [Biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }})

---

_Big Pharma_{: .glossary #big-pharma}
: A collective term for the massive, multinational pharmaceutical corporations that *dominate* modern global healthcare. While this industry has produced undeniable, *life-saving* _scientific advancements_{: .cool}, it fundamentally operates as a *profit-driven institution* within modern hierarchical systems. From an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) perspective, Big Pharma frequently _exploits_{: .warn} [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) by *pathologising and over-medicating* [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) human behaviours and emotional distress, *prioritizing* **highly profitable**, lifelong symptom management _over_{: .warn} addressing the systemic, environmental *root causes* of illness. By wielding *immense financial capital* to shape research, public policy, and modern medicine, it frequently acts as a structural [lock-in]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) that *prioritizes corporate growth* over holistic [human flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing).

---

_Biological Needs_{: .glossary #biological-needs}
: The foundational, non-negotiable [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) _requirements_{: .bold} for human *physical* and *psychological* [flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing) forged by **millions of years** of [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}). These extend beyond basic food and shelter to include [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy), _robust social connection_{: .cool}, physical movement and touch, play, [sexuality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sexuality) and *clear communal purpose*—needs _frequently_{: .warn} [suppressed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression) by modern cultural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Biology_{: .glossary #biology}
