: The scientifically discredited practice of *trying to alter* the genetic quality of a human population through _forced_{: .warn} or controlled reproduction, historically driven by [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [bias]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) and pseudoscience.

**See also:** [The Eugenics Movement]({{ "/case-studies/pseudoscience/eugenics/" | relative_url }})

---

_Evidence-Based_{: .glossary}
: A *rigorous approach* to decision-making, policy, or belief-formation that relies strictly on verified data, [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) research, and [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}), rather than on [tradition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#tradition), intuition, or unproven assumptions.

---

_Evolutionary History_{: .glossary #evolutionary-history}
: The *vast chronological timeline* detailing the biological and cognitive development of a species. For humans, this encompasses **millions of years** of [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) as hunter-gatherers, hardwiring our brains with instincts and [biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) that frequently _misfire_{: .warn} in the unnatural, constructed environments of the modern world.

---

_Evolutionary Psychology_{: .glossary #evolutionary-psychology}
: A [scientific approach]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) that explains [human behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), emotions, and thoughts as [adaptations]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#adaptation) shaped by our ancestral past to solve problems of [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) and reproduction.

---

_Evolved Instincts_{: .glossary}
: The [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate), hardwired cognitive and behavioral impulses shaped by millions of years of [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}). Optimized for [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) in ancestral hunter-gatherer environments, these biological drives—such as tribal loyalty, [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }})-seeking, and a [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of the [out-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }})—often misfire or create systemic vulnerabilities when deployed in the *vastly different context* of modern, complex societies.

---

_Evolved Needs_{: .glossary #evolved-needs}
: The basic biological and psychological requirements humans have developed over **millions of years** of [evolution]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) (such as the need for *social connection*, [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}), or physical safety).

---

_Evolving Capacity_{:.glossary #evolving-capacity}
: The [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) *biological reality* of human neurological and psychological _development_{: .bold}. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), the transition to adulthood was **not an event**, but *a continuous gradient*. Within the _horizontal_{: .cool} structure of [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan), [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) organically *built competence* through constant, *real-world* _participation_{: .bold}, *age-disparate* _peering_{: .bold}, and *gradually scaled* _responsibilities_{: .bold}. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), and in particular following the _Industrial Revolution_{: .bold}, [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) _segregated_{: .warn} youth from the community, locking them inside *highly* _managed_{: .warn} institutions, like schools and the [isolated nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}). To justify this [architecture of isolation]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/housing-crisis/" | relative_url }}), the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) invented a deeply [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) legal fiction: the belief that a human being remains entirely incompetent until an arbitrary bureaucratic threshold (such as an 18th birthday), at which point full adulthood is magically switched on. This culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) framework causes a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). By denying youth *genuine* [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) and **stripping** them of [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [risk-taking]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#risk-taking), the system actively _stunts_{: .warn} the development of the prefrontal cortex, _generating the very immaturity the state claims to be protecting them from_{: .mark}. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires wiping the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By assessing human growth strictly from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we must recognise that decision-making and resilience are not granted by [laws]({{ "/case-studies/law/theory/" | relative_url }}); they are [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) biological capacities that can only be forged through active, continuous engagement with the real world.

---

_Expendability_{: .glossary #expendability}
: The implicit, systemic view held by large hierarchical structures (such as empires, militaries, or modern economies) that individual human lives are replaceable commodities. It relies heavily on culturally normalized [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) of [self-sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }}) to *convince the vulnerable* to willingly _exchange_{: .warn} their health, labour, or lives for _the benefit of the elite_{: .mark}.

---

_Experiment_{: .glossary #experiment}
: A controlled, structured [scientific procedure]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) undertaken to [test]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) a [hypothesis]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hypothesis), discover an unknown effect, or establish a law of nature by systematically manipulating variables and measuring the [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) outcomes. In a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), the experiment is the ultimate arbiter of truth. Because our brains evolved to rely on rapid [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics), intuition, and [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) for immediate [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival), we naturally confuse [correlation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#correlation) with causation or blindly accept the [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) of our [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). A rigorously designed experiment *actively bypasses these biological flaws* by forcing our [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) beliefs to collide with physical reality. It is the **critical mechanism** that prevents [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas) and unquestioned [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) from _masquerading_{: .warn} as fact, ensuring our conclusions are grounded strictly in [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) reality _rather than what a society merely agrees to believe_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Experiment]({{ "/case-studies/law/theory/adaptive-law/" | relative_url }})

---

_Exploitation_{: .glossary #exploitation}
: The systemic act of *utilizing human vulnerability*, [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) biases, or the natural environment for outsized **elite gain**. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), hierarchical systems _normalised_{: .bold} the extraction of surplus labour and resources from the powerless to benefit those controlling the societal [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Expression_{: .glossary #expression}
: The [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) *externalisation of human emotion*, thought, and identity. Modern constructed systems frequently _restrict_{: .warn} expression to enforce [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity), whereas our [evolutionary history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) relied on authentic, unfiltered emotional expression for [co-regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation) and *deep social bonding* within [the clan]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}). In our ancestral environment, openly sharing joy, [distress]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#distress-and-confusion), or [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) was *not a weakness*, but a _vital biological mechanism_{: .bold} that signaled the group to provide *immediate mutual aid*. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), massive, hierarchical civilizations began to view [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) emotional variance as a _threat_{: .warn} to [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}). Today, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and bureaucratic institutions actively [pathologise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#pathologize) this biological need, demanding _emotional suppression_{: .warn} and rigid [standardisation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation) in schools and workplaces. This severe [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) cuts us off from genuine [connection]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#connection), _driving epidemic levels of psychological alienation_{: .mark}.

---

_Facts and Reality_{: .glossary #facts-and-reality}
: The [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) *state of the physical universe* that **exists completely independently** of human [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), [cultural narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or societal [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus). While modern post-Agricultural societies heavily rely on and enforce [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [myths]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) as "truth", biological and physical reality remains unyielding to *emotional appeals*, **tribal pressure**, or structural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Factual Claim_{: .glossary #factual-claim}
: A [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}), [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) assertion about reality that can be empirically supported or falsified using the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}). Unlike _cultura_{: .bold}l [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) or [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) beliefs, a factual claim relies entirely on [measurable evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) and remains true or false regardless of personal *feelings*, political [ideologies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology), or tribal [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus).

---

_Fairness_{: .glossary #fairness}
: The [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate), [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) _instinct_{: .bold} that *helped early human primates to survive* in tight-knit [groups]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}). These traits form the biological, [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) foundation of what we intuitively recognise as [justice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#justice-and-injustice) today. Rather than competitive, hyper-individualism, our [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) baseline relies heavily on *mutual aid*, equitable *resource sharing*, and [horizontal justice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#horizontal-justice) to maintain group cohesion.

---

_Fallacies_{: .glossary #fallacies}
: Specific _flaws_{: .warn} or _traps_{: .warn} in reasoning that *render an argument logically invalid*, even if the final conclusion happens to feel **emotionally appealing** or true.

**See also:** [Fallacies]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/logical-fallacies/" | relative_url }})

---

_False Certainty_{: .glossary #false-certainty}
: A *heavily engineered psychological* [illusion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#illusion) where a [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) or institution presents an [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) as an absolute, unquestionable fact. By exploiting our hardwired [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) craving for simple answers and simple causality, authorities use relentless repetition, [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media) [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing), and the backing of a [secular priesthood]({{ "/guide/obstacles/secular-priesthood/" | relative_url }}) to *completely* _bypass_{: .warn} the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}), turning *unevidenced* _assumptions_{: .warn} into rigid [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma).

---

_False Negative_{: .glossary #false-negative}
: An *error in perception* where an individual fails to detect a real threat or pattern (e.g., mistaking a stalking predator for the wind). Because this error often resulted in death for [early humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#early-humans), [natural selection]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural-selection) heavily penalised it.

---

_False Positive_{: .glossary #false-positive}
: An *error in perception* where an individual detects a threat or pattern that *isn't actually there* (e.g., mistaking the wind for a predator). [Evolution]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) favoured brains prone to this error because running away from the wind is much safer than ignoring a real predator, **inadvertently** *priming the human mind* for _supernatural_{: .bold} [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs).

---

_Falsifiability_{: .glossary #falsifiability}
: The *logical possibility* that an _assertion_{: .bold}, [hypothesis]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hypothesis), or [theory]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#theory) can be *shown to be false* by an observation or [experiment]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#experiment); if a claim is not [falsifiable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}), it is _not_{: .warn} _scientific_{: .bold} and can never be _evidence-based_{: .bold}.

---

_Fear_{: .glossary #fear}
: An [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}), acute biological [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) _mechanism_{: .bold} "designed" by [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to alert [early humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#early-humans) to **immediate physical danger**. However, because our ancestors *could only survive* through strict [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) within their tribe, _social fears_{: .bold}—specifically the terror of [shame]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shame), rejection, and ostracism—evolved to be *just as intensely powerful* as the fear of predators. To the ancestral [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}), social isolation meant certain death. Today, this is a *profound* [mismatch]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mismatches). In modern, [coercive]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) institutions or [isolated nuclear families]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}), this [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) fear of abandonment is frequently weaponised as a tool for [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}) and compliance. At a societal scale, politicians, [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media), and [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [true believers]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#true-believers) _hijack_{: .warn} this same biological terror—either by stoking fear of the [out-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) or threatening in-group ostracism—to _bypass_{: .warn} [rational]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rational) [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}), short-circuit the demand for [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), and **manufacture unquestioning compliance** to their [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}).

**See also:** [Fear-Mongering]({{ "/guide/obstacles/fear-mongering/" | relative_url }})

---

_Finance_{: .glossary #finance}
: A highly abstract, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) system for managing wealth, debt, and exchange. Because it operates *entirely independently* of [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) physical resources or biological limits, it frequently incentivises _short-term extraction_{: .warn} and [exploitation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation), rewarding behaviours that damage the real, physical environment.

---

_Fluctuation_{: .glossary #fluctuation}
: The [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), rhythmic cycling of biological human needs and behaviours—such as the balance between rest and effort, or solitude and [intimacy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation). Modern [standardisation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation) **attempts to erase** _natural_{: .cool} biological fluctuation in favour of *relentless, linear productivity*.

---

_Fragmentation_{: .glossary #fragmentation}
: The *flawed methodological tendency*—often seen in [modern psychology]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) and _institutions_{: .bold}—to _divide_{: .warn} complex, interconnected human traits, behaviours, or social problems into *isolated* "syndromes", [disorders]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#disorder) or categories _without_{: .warn} addressing the cohesive, underlying biological and [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) systems that drive them.

---

_Framing_{: .glossary #framing}
: The *deliberate* psychological and rhetorical presentation of an issue, [story]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or fact to **encourage** a *specific interpretation* while _obscuring_{: .warn} [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) reality. Because the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) evolved to be a **cognitive miser**—*saving energy* by relying on rapid [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics) rather than _deep analysis_{: .cool}—_it is highly vulnerable to how information is packaged_{: .mark}. Framing effectively *sets the invisible boundaries of a debate*; if you accept the frame, you have already conceded the _conclusion_{: .bold}. By _highlighting_{: .warn} certain *emotional triggers* and _hiding_{: .warn} *contradictory data*, framing bypasses [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) and manipulates [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) to _steer public perception_{: .mark}. It is the primary tool used by [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media), institutions, and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) to protect entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) and legitimise intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }})—often by *cleverly blurring the line* between what is merely socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) and what is actually [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural). In a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), *actively dismantling* the **inherited** _frame_{: .bold} and returning to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) is the *mandatory first step* before analyzing the actual [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Freeloading_{: .glossary #freeloading}
: Taking the benefits of group [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) without _contributing_{: .bold} *one's fair share*. In our [evolutionary history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), our ancestors developed **moral emotions** and [horizontal justice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#horizontal-justice) specifically to *identify* and *punish* this [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) to ensure group [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival).

---

_Free Speech_{: .glossary #free-speech}
: The fundamental right to _articulate opinions and ideas_{: .cool} without [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of retaliation, censorship, or legal sanction; an absolute _prerequisite_{: .bold} for [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) and the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) to function.

**See also:** [Free Speech]({{ "/case-studies/politics/miscelleneous/free-speech/" | relative_url }})

---

_Gender Roles_{: .glossary #gender-role}
: The *chronic psychological and systemic friction* caused by **forcing human** [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) into rigid, post-Agricultural gender [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). While our species [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to utilize the flexible, cooperative labour and shared authority of both sexes, modern [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) **heavily restricts** _human potential_{: .cool} by confining individuals to [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), gendered expectations (e.g., women exclusively as [domestic]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/" | relative_url }}) caregivers; men exclusively as providers). While modern Western societies frequently _claim_{: .warn} this [mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) is **"solved"** through legal equality, women essentially *won the right to participate* in an **industrial wage system** that is still structurally _dependent_{: .bold} on the [isolated nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}). Because the ancestral [alloparenting]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#alloparenting) network remains dismantled, this "progress" merely shifted the [mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) from *strict domestic confinement* to systemic, [exhausting burnout]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#burnout).

---

_Genetic Legacy_{: .glossary #genetic-legacy}
: The *continuation* of an organism's specific DNA into future generations. Crucially, from an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) perspective, this is *not strictly limited* to an individual's immediate ability to reproduce; it is about ensuring the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of the *broader, shared gene pool*. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) survived in small, interdependent foraging bands where almost everyone was *closely related*. Therefore, traits like kin [selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}), profound [empathy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empathy), physiological [co-regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation), and even fatal [self-sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }}) _evolved_{: .bold} specifically because they aided the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of *other* present or future reproducers within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) who might very well share the same genes. This *deeply ingrained* biological drive to protect [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) legacy is one of our *most powerful instincts*. However, in massive [post-transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) societies, this creates a *highly* _exploitable_{: .warn} [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Because our brains cannot instinctively distinguish between a literal genetic tribe and an *artificial one*, coercive institutions (Royalty, Religious Priesthoods, etc) and nowadays the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) actively _hijack_{: .warn} this _noble_{: .bold} *instinct*. By using manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and manufactured [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), they _redirect_{: .bold} our *ancient willingness to sacrifice ourselves* away from our *actual* _kin_{: .bold} and _community_{: .bold}, _demanding we lay down our lives_{: .mark} and resources to serve abstract ideologies, nation-states, or the economic outputs of deeply entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins). Viewing this vulnerability through a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) strips away the emotional [manipulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#manipulation), allowing us to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and recognise when our biological loyalty is being _weaponised_{: .warn} against us.

---

_Genetics_{: .glossary #genetics}
: The [scientific study]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) of *heredity* and the *variation* of inherited characteristics in living organisms through DNA.

**See also:** [Nature and Nurture]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/genetics/" | relative_url }})

---

_Groupthink_{: .glossary #groupthink}
: A *psychological phenomenon* where the desire for harmony, [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity), and tribal loyalty within a group causes its members to *silently self-censor* their doubts, leading to irrational or [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) decision-making. From an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) perspective, groupthink is *not a flaw of intelligence*, but a deeply _hardwired_{: .bold} [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) mechanism. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), maintaining *absolute cohesion* within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) was a matter of life and death; challenging [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) consensus risked *deadly* _ostracization_{: .warn}. The ancestral [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) evolved to *prioritize social belonging* over [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), this biological drive has become a highly exploitable [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). In large modern institutions, groupthink actively _protects_{: .warn} destructive societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}). The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) relies on this phenomenon to *enforce compliance*, knowing that individuals will naturally _suppress_{: .bold} their own [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) due to an [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of being [shamed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shame) or expelled from the group. Breaking free from groupthink requires true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). It demands applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to _intentionally disrupt false harmony_{: .mark}, wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), and insist that all decisions be grounded strictly in [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), regardless of the *social friction* it inevitably causes.

---

_Guilt_{: .glossary #guilt}
: An [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) *moral emotion* that originally helped individuals recognise when they had harmed their kin group, *prompting reconciliation*. Today, it is routinely _weaponised_{: .warn} by religions, institutions, and [nuclear families]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) to *enforce* unnatural [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity).

---

_Harm Reduction_{: .glossary #harm-reduction}
: An [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) *pragmatic approach* to social policy, medicine, and [justice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#justice-and-injustice) that prioritises minimising the negative, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) consequences of [human behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) rather than _imposing_{: .warn} moral _judgments_{: .bold} or demanding impossible, [dogmatic]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) compliance to [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}). Evolutionarily, our brains are _hardwired_{: .bold} for *moral policing*; in a highly vulnerable ancestral band, *unpredictable* _deviations_{: .bold} from [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) rules could threaten collective [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival), triggering a deep [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) that resulted in the *harsh punishment* or ostracism of rule-breakers. In modern, massive [post-transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) societies, this _instinct_{: .bold} has become *a deeply destructive* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Instead of addressing *the systemic causes of suffering*, coercive institutions and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) frequently [weaponise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) this ancient [moral outrage]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/moral-outrage/" | relative_url }}) to _punish_{: .warn} **human struggles**—such as addiction, [mental health]({{ "/case-studies/health/mental-health/" | relative_url }}) crises, sexual orientation or non-conformity to the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). They demand *absolute adherence* to artificial [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) of "healthy thinking" or "personal responsibility". **Harm reduction** actively _rejects_{: .bold} this tribal moralising. By applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), it returns to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and asks a simple, [first-principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) question: *What actually improves lives and reduces* [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) *suffering?* It _abandons_{: .bold} intersubjective [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) in favour of measurable, [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) outcomes, proving that _protecting human_{: .mark} [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) _is far more important_{: .mark} than _enforcing_{: .warn} social [normality]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Healthy_{: .glossary #healthy}
: In an [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), *biological sense*, a state of physical [flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing) and [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) fitness. However, in modern psychological and behavioural contexts, "healthy" is *heavily* [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction)—often used as a *synonym for compliance* with the current cultural "[normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }})", effectively [medicalising]({{ "/case-studies/pseudoscience/medicalisation/" | relative_url }}) or _pathologising_{: .warn} [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) human behaviours that simply fail to [conform]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) to *institutional expectations*.

---

_Hero_{: .glossary #hero}
: A *culturally constructed* _label_{: .bold} frequently awarded to individuals who exhibit extreme [self-sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }}). While originally rooted in the **biological instinct** to *protect close kin* (and hence protect [genetic legacy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#genetic-legacy)), modern hierarchical systems heavily [weaponise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) the concept of the _"hero"_{: .bold} (such as the martyred worker or the fallen soldier) to praise *individual* _depletion_{: .warn} rather than fix the underlying **systemic failures** and [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) that required the sacrifice.

---

_Heuristics_{: .glossary #heuristics}
: *Deeply ingrained* mental shortcuts _hardwired_{: .bold} into the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) by **millions of years** of [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}). In the high-stakes, physically immediate environment of our hunter-gatherer ancestors, [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics) were *highly adaptive*, enabling split-second [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) decisions without the need for *slow, energy-consuming logic* (such as *instantly trusting* kin or *assuming* a rustling bush was a predator). However, in the vast, abstract, and **artificial complexity** of [post-Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) societies, these ancient mental shortcuts create *severe* [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Today, they are routinely _hijacked_{: .warn} by modern _institutions_{: .bold}, political _propagandists_{: .bold}, and algorithmic _media_{: .bold} to *bypass* [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}), [exploit]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}), and manufacture _blind compliance_{: .warn} to [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Hierarchies_{: .glossary #hierarchies}
: Systems of *ranking and organizing* individuals or groups based on [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}), [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}), or [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority). While basic dominance [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) have deep [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) roots in primate behaviour, the *rigid, systemic, and often* [oppressive]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#oppression) socio-economic [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) that define post [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) [history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) are largely _unevidenced_{: .warn} [social constructions]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction), engineered to *manage surplus resources* and maintain [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Hijacked Instincts_{: .glossary #hijacked-instincts}
: *Hardwired* [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) behaviours (like kin loyalty, [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear), or [self-sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }})) that are *deliberately* _manipulated_{: .warn} by modern _institutions_{: .bold}, _corporations_{: .bold}, or _states_{: .bold} to serve *culturally constructed* [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas) rather than the biological [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of the individual or their _true community_{: .cool}.

---

_Hindsight Bias_{: .glossary #hindsight-bias}
: The *psychological illusion* that a past event was *perfectly predictable* after it has already occurred. This mental _glitch_{: .warn} protects the human brain's *deep* [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) craving for [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty) and *pattern recognition*, falsely _convincing__{: .bold} individuals that they *intrinsically understand* complex modern systems, which in turn breeds []({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#overconfidence) and *prevents meaningful inquiry* into **actual systemic failures**.

---

_Horizontal Justice_{: .glossary #horizontal-justice}
: The [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), *peer-to-peer system* of managing conflict and enforcing group [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) found in [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) early [human societies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#anthropology), driven by [reputation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reputation-tracking), [empathy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empathy), and _mutual accountability_{: .cool} rather than centralized [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority).

---

_Humane_{: .glossary #humane}
: An approach to systems, laws, and social structures that *prioritises* [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) human [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing), [empathy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empathy), and [harm reduction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#harm-reduction). A truly _humane society_{: .cool} *aligns* its _institutions_{: .bold} with our *hardwired* [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) needs, *rejecting* unevidenced [dogmas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) and _punitive_{: .warn} [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) that *artificially cause suffering*.

---

_Human Flourishing_{: .glossary #human-flourishing}
: The *optimal state* of physical, psychological, and social [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing). From an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) perspective, [flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing) is *only possible* when a [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) _actively aligns_{: .cool} its systems and structures with our [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _biological needs_{: .bold} and [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) [human nature]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-nature), rather than _forcing_{: .warn} individuals to adapt to unnatural [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) and _arbitrary_{: .bold} [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies).

---

_Humanism_{: .glossary #humanism}
: A [rationalistic worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) that *prioritises* human [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing), agency, and [flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing), relying on **reason and science** rather than _divine_{: .bold} or _supernatural_{: .bold} intervention to solve human problems.

**See also:** [Secular Humanism]({{ "/case-studies/morality/humanism/" | relative_url }})

---

_Human Nature_{: .glossary #human-nature}
: The *baseline suite* of biological, cognitive, behavioural and psychological _traits_{: .bold} that are *evolutionarily hardwired* into [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens). Shaped by **millions of years** of [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) during our hunter-gatherer past, these [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) predispositions—such as [tribalism]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}), [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }})-seeking, and pattern recognition—form the [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _foundation_{: .bold} of [human behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), *directly challenging* the notion that the human mind is entirely a cultural "blank slate". The study of human nature is called [Anthropology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#anthropology)

---

_Human Needs_{: .glossary #human-needs}
: The *foundational* biological and psychological _requirements_{: .bold} forged by **millions of years** of [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}). These include physical *movement*, *purpose*, deep *social connection*, [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy), natural *light* and restorative *rest*. Modern post-agricultural societies frequently _suppress_{: .warn} these [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) needs, *attempting to replace them* with artificial consumerism or _bureaucratic busywork_{: .bold}, leading to systemic _distress_{: .warn}.

---

_Human Virtue_{: .glossary #human-virtue}
: Behaviours and traits *culturally celebrated* as morally "superior". From an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) perspective, many *so-called virtues* in the modern world (like relentless labour, unquestioning [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience), or endless [self-sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }})) are actually [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) constructs designed to _enforce_{: .warn} compliance and benefit the dominant [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}) structure, actively _hijacking_{: .warn} [natural instincts]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) for the system's gain.

---

_Hypothesis_{: .glossary #hypothesis}
: A *proposed, provisional explanation* for an observed _phenomenon_{: .bold} or a specific question, formulated based on limited initial [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}). To be *scientifically valid*, a [hypothesis]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hypothesis) must be [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) (aka *falsifiable*), serving as a starting point for *rigorous investigation* rather than an _unchallengeable_{: .warn} assertion of truth.

---

_Identity_{: .glossary #identity}
: A person's *conception of self*. For [early humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#early-humans), identity was *naturally embedded* within tight-knit, cooperative _kin networks_{: .bold}. In modern, atomised societies, identity has become a highly individualised and often _fragile_{: .warn} social construct, frequently *outsourced* to [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) labels, political tribes, or economic output (such as job titles) to _compensate for the loss of genuine_{: .mark} [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) _community_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Identity Politics]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/identity/" | relative_url }})

---

_Ideology_{: .glossary #ideology}
: A _rigid_{: .warn} system of *ideas and ideals*, often forming the basis of a political, economic, [religious]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion), or [secular priesthood]({{ "/guide/obstacles/secular-priesthood/" | relative_url }}) [worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#worldview).

---

_Idolising_{: .glossary #idolising}
: The act of treating a [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) concept, [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or institution (such as the [nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}), a political leader, or a [traditional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#tradition) law) with *uncritical reverence*. In an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) worldview, [idolising]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#idolising) structural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) _prevents_{: .warn} [rational]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rational) inquiry, systemic _reform_{: .bold}, and the pursuit of [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [human flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing).

---

_Illusion_{: .glossary #illusion}

A *deceptive or distorted perception* of reality where the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) misinterprets sensory inputs, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}), or social [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), mistaking a [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) impression for an **absolute truth**.

---

_Illusion of Modern Equity_{: .glossary #illusion-of-modern-equity}
: The [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) Western [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) that legal [emancipation]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/emancipation/" | relative_url }}) and workforce participation have *completely resolved* historical gender [oppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#oppression). From an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) perspective, true equity requires _aligning_{: .cool} our social structures with human [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology). _Forcing_{: .warn} individuals to navigate a highly competitive [capitalist]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#capitalism) economy while simultaneously absorbing the massive [caregiving]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#alloparenting) deficit of the [collapsed clan] is **not objective flourishing**; it is simply a **modernized, rebranded** structural [lock-in]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Inclusive Fitness (Kin Selection)_{: .glossary #inclusive-fitness-kin-selection}
: An [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) concept explaining that an organism's _genetic success_{: .bold} is increased **not just** from *its own reproduction*, but from the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) and reproduction of its **close relatives**. It is the biological foundation of [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [self-sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }}), mathematically ensuring that shared DNA survives within the broader gene pool *even if the individual perishes*.

---

_Independent Thought_{: .glossary #independent-thought}
: The **deliberate rejection** of inherited social [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and modern [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). In the ancestral environment, breaking [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) with [the tribe]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) *often meant death*. Today, true [independent thought]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) requires _actively overriding_{: .bold} that primal biological [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of ostracism in order to evaluate modern, [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) systems against [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) reality and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Industrialisation_{: .glossary #industrialisation}
: A massive, *relatively recent* technological and social acceleration of the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}). It permanently _severed_{: .warn} the majority of human beings from [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) environments, replacing *seasonal, self-directed labour* with rigid, clock-bound [standardisation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation), mass production, and the **artificial rhythms** of the factory and office.

---

_Inequality_{: .glossary #inequality}
: The *profoundly unbalanced and artificial* distribution of _resources_{: .bold}, _opportunities_{: .bold}, [rights]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/rights/" | relative_url }}), or [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}) within a society. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) and earlier humans evolved in _fiercely egalitarian_{: .cool} hunter-gatherer bands where *hoarding was actively punished* and deep [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) was the foundation of [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). In our ancestral environment, severe [inequality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inequality) was **biologically unviable**; anyone who attempted to _monopolize_{: .warn} resources faced *immediate exile or execution*. However, the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition) fundamentally _ruptured_{: .warn} this dynamic, introducing storable surpluses, private property, and *rigid social hierarchies*. Today, extreme economic and social stratification is a devastating [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) that _triggers_{: .warn} *chronic physiological stress* and societal [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction). Because severe [inequality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inequality) contradicts our biologically [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) instincts, modern institutions and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) *must expend massive effort* to _maintain it_{: .bold}. They use clever [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }})—such as [meritocracy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#meritocracy)," "divine right", or "economic necessity"—to convince us that extreme stratification is both inevitable and [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}). Dismantling this systemic [lock-in]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview). By returning to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and examining the [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can expose extreme [inequality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inequality) not as an immutable law of human nature, but as an _artificial design flaw that objectively damages human_{: .mark} [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology).

**See also**: [Wealth and Inequality]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/wealth-and-inequality/" | relative_url }}) and [Understanding Inequalities]({{ "/case-studies/politics/miscelleneous/understanding-inequalities/" | relative_url }})

---

_Inertia_{: .glossary #inertia}
: A profound, *systemic tendency to do nothing* or to _remain unchanged_{: .bold}; the immense, passive resistance of individuals and societies to progress, reform, or the integration of new information. From an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) perspective, inertia is a **biological feature, not a bug**. The [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) evolved to be a *cognitive miser*. For 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), *conserving metabolic energy* was crucial for [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). Questioning established routines or deviating from the [in-group's]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) _traditions_{: .bold} required *massive calorie expenditure* and introduced *unnecessary risk*; if a shared habit kept [the tribe]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) alive yesterday, the safest biological _bet_{: .bold} was to repeat it *blindly* today. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), however, this [innate preference]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) for the [status quo]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#status-quo) became a *catastrophic* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). In our complex modern world, inertia _traps_{: .warn} us within deeply [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins). The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and coercive institutions actively rely on this mental _lethargy_{: .bold}, knowing that humans will endure immense systemic harm simply because it feels more culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) and less terrifying than _the effort required for systemic change_{: .mark}. They use intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to dress up this *passive momentum* as [tradition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#tradition)", **"stability"**, or **"common sense"**. Shattering inertia requires the radical, *metabolically expensive act* of intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By *consciously applying* a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) and returning to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), we force ourselves to _evaluate our societal structures not by their historical momentum_{: .mark}, but strictly by their alignment with [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) and [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Information Overload_{: .glossary #information-overload}
: An extreme [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). For **millions of years**, human data inputs were *strictly limited* to immediate physical environments and small tribal networks. Today, the modern digital and media landscape _floods_{: .warn} our ancient neurobiology with **endless artificial stimuli**, *hijacking our attention spans* and _paralyzing_{: .bold} our capacity for [rational]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rational) analysis.

---

_Innate_{: .glossary #innate}
: Traits, behaviours, or instincts (like [altruism]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#altruism), play, or curiosity) that are _hardwired_{: .bold} *into human* [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) through **millions of years** of [evolution]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}), existing _independently_{: .bold} of cultural conditioning or [formal education]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/education/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Innovation_{: .glossary #innovation}
: The *profound human capacity* for problem-solving, adaptation, and technological creation, driven by our [highly evolved brains]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}). While it has vastly improved medical and material realities, innovation under modern market forces is frequently _misdirected_{: .warn} toward manufacturing consumer **anxiety**, capturing **attention**, or **designing weapons**, rather than solving [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) human needs.

---

_Instinct_{: .bullet #instinct}
: The _hardwired_{: .bold}, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) biological survival scripts and behavioral drives forged over millions of years of human evolution. For **over 95% **of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), our _baseline instincts_{: .cool}—such as the drive for eating, shelter, sexuality, continuous physical movement, intense curiosity, autonomous risk-taking, and the desperate need for horizontal integration within [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan)—were _perfectly calibrated_{: .cool} to ensure [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) and [human flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), modern society _trapped_{: .warn} the human animal inside an *unnatural* [architecture of isolation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#architecture-of-isolation), demanding strict [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience) to sedentary, top-down environments. When our biology predictably _executes_{: .terminal} its ancestral _programming_{: .terminal} in these culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) settings (e.g., a child resisting coercion in a classroom, or a traumatized brain triggering hyper-vigilance, aggression or substance misuse), it creates a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Rather than acknowledging the structural failure of the environment, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) routinely [medicalises]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#medicalisation) or criminalizes these instincts, using [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) made-up [labels]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#labeling) to drug or punish the individual. Applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) demands that we wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/guide/clean-slate/" | relative_url }}) and stop prosecuting biology, recognising _instinct_{: .cool} not as a "moral failing" or a "clinical" [disorder]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#disorder), but as an [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) reality _that must be accommodated by our social structures_{: .mark}.

---

_Intellectual Humility_{: .glossary #intellectual-humility}
: The [rational]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rational), [scientific willingness]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) to *admit that your* [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), knowledge, or [worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#worldview) *might be incomplete or incorrect*, combined with an openness to *revising your stance* when presented with superior, [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}). From an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) perspective, true intellectual humility is a **profoundly unnatural state**. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) relied on *absolute loyalty* to the shared myths of the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). The ancestral [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) did not evolve to seek [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth; it evolved to _crave_{: .bold} [certainty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#certainty) and social cohesion. Openly doubting [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) _consensus_{: .bold}, or **admitting your own ignorance**, was a dangerous gamble that risked *deadly ostracism*. Today, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), this biological _aversion_{: .warn} to admitting error is a catastrophic [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). It actively _traps_{: .warn} us in [groupthink]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#groupthink) and allows the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) to enforce unquestioned [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma), knowing our brains will fiercely defend societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) just to _avoid the physiological discomfort of being wrong_{: .mark}. _Intellectual humility_{: .cool} is the absolute prerequisite for true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), we must actively override our ancient [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of uncertainty, *intentionally decouple our identity* from our inherited [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), and wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). It is the radical, *metabolically expensive commitment* to aligning our minds with [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) and [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) reality, no matter the social cost.

**See also:** [Intellectual Humility]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/intellectual-humility/" | relative_url }})

---

_Intersubjective Phenomena_{: .glossary #intersubjective-phenomena}
: [Social constructions]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction)—such as money, nations, religions, corporations, and laws—that exist purely because a massive group of human beings *collectively agree to believe* in them. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) relied on *direct, face-to-face trust* within small, intimate foraging bands. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), our species faced the unprecedented challenge of **organizing anonymous strangers**. Intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) became the ultimate *evolutionary hack*, allowing us to scale [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) globally by _rallying massive populations around shared fictions_{: .mark}. The danger, however, is deeply biological: the ancestral [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) is *not equipped to distinguish* between [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) physical reality (like gravity or [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology)) and intersubjective reality (like the economy or political borders). This creates a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Because we instinctively _conflate_{: .warn} the two, we treat entirely artificial, historically [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) systems as though they are _immutable_{: .bold}, [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) laws. The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [True Believers]({{ "/guide/obstacles/true-believers/" | relative_url }}) aggressively *exploit this cognitive blind spot*, using moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and tribal [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) loyalties to _calcify_{: .warn} these useful cultural tools into oppressive, unquestionable [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). True intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to **sever** this [illusion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#illusion). By returning to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and examining [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can *demystify these constructs*—recognizing that while we cannot change the [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) laws of physics, intersubjective phenomena are merely _human software_{: .terminal} that can, and must, be rewritten when they no longer serve human [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing).

**See also:** [Objective vs. Subjective vs. Intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }})

---

_IPP Sentences_{: .glossary #ipp-sentences}
: A defunct (but still functionally lingering) Imprisonment for [Public Protection]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#protection-and-protectors): UK sentencing structure that allowed for indefinite detention based on the unscientific, [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) prediction of future **"risk"**, violating the [core principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) of [testability]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) and [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) [justice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#justice-and-injustice).

---

_Intensive Parenting_{: .glossary #intensive-parenting}
: A highly *unnatural*, modern [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) norm that *demands* one or two isolated adults provide constant, *hyper-vigilant supervision*, [education]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/education/" | relative_url }}), and emotional regulation for a child. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) and earlier humans raised children through "alloparenting" within *deeply interdependent* foraging bands. The immense biological burden of care, play, and instruction was _shared_{: .cool} across dozens of [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) members, granting [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) necessary [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) while providing [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) with a *resilient, distributed web of attachment*. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), the _severing_{: .warn} of community networks and the eventual rise of the [isolated nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) created a *brutal* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). To *mask the structural failure* of a [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) that offers no communal support, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) of modern [parenting]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) gurus and institutions actively _weaponises_{: .warn} guilt and [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear). They use manipulated moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to push the [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) that absolute parental [self-sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }}) is a *biological duty*, when it is actually a historically recent, culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) invention. This _structurally impossible_{: .bold} [expectation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#tolerance) replaces the vital [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) of the ancestral village with chronic parental [burnout]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#burnout) and _extreme_{: .warn} **developmental pressure** on [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children), who naturally evolved for *autonomous play* rather than *relentless micromanagement*. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By stripping away this oppressive [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) and looking at child-rearing from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can **stop pathologising ourselves** for failing at an empirical *impossibility* and _recognise that human_{: .mark} [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) _fundamentally requires a tribe to raise a child_{: .mark}.

---

_Kin Selection_{: .glossary #kin-selection}
: The *foundational* [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) mechanism explaining why organisms *willingly sacrifice* themselves for others. It dictates that an individual will act altruistically toward close relatives to ensure the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) and propagation of their *shared DNA* within the gene pool. For **millions of years**, this _hardwired_{: .bold} biological [instinct]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#instinct) was the engine of mutual aid and cohesion within tight-knit hunter-gatherer bands. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), modern hierarchical systems have systematically _hijacked_{: .warn} this powerful drive, *manipulating* humans into sacrificing their health, labour, and lives for abstract, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) entities like corporations, nations, and *unevidenced ideologies*. See [Inclusive Fitness]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inclusive-fitness-kin-selection)

---

_Justice and Injustice_{: .glossary #justice-and-injustice}
: While frequently treated as purely cultural or philosophical constructs, these concepts are *deeply rooted* in our [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) biological instincts for fairness, [reciprocity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reciprocity), and strict [egalitarianism]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian). For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) in small foraging bands required *absolute interdependence*; our brains developed a _hyper-sensitivity_{: .bold} to resource hoarding, free-riding, or arbitrary dominance, instinctively viewing them as *existential threats* to the [in-group's]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the rise of massive, stratified societies created a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Today, our innate biological drive for fairness is routinely _hijacked_{: .warn} by coercive institutions and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood). They use manipulated moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }})—such as [meritocracy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#meritocracy)," "law and order", or "karmic debt"—to convince us that profoundly _unjust_{: .warn}, artificial societal [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) are actually socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) or *morally required*. In an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) framework, systemic injustice occurs when these unevidenced [social constructions]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) are used to enforce [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [oppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#oppression). Achieving true justice requires intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), we can strip away cultural [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) and evaluate systems based strictly on [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) outcomes and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), _dismantling artificial hierarchies and treating individuals based on verifiable facts_{: .mark} rather than tribal [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Labeling_{: .glossary #labeling}
: The **deliberate** use of [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) language to permanently brand individuals—particularly those reacting to severe [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }})—as fundamentally defective, "deviant", or morally corrupt. Within the ancestral safety of [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan), harmful actions were addressed through immediate, [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _restitution_{: .cool}, but an individual's core identity remained deeply integrated within the group. Today, however, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and especially the (tabloid) media utilises labeling as a primary mechanism of top-down social control. When a person's [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) predictably rebels against culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) structural failures, the state applies reductive labels ("criminal", "delinquent", "addict") to individualise the [blame]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/blame/" | relative_url }}) and protect the [status quo]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#status-quo). This branding **actively strips away** the individual's _pro-social scaffolding_{: .cool}, forcing them into a self-fulfilling sociological feedback loop. Ostracized by the wider community, the labeled individual internalizes this new identity and escalates their anti-social [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) behaviours to find belonging elsewhere—a destructive process known directly as [deviance amplification]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#deviance-amplification). A [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) demands we wipe the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), recognising that human beings are complex biological organisms responding to their environments, _not static caricatures defined by the punitive vocabulary of the state_{: .mark}.

---

_Laws_{: .glossary #laws}
: [Intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) agreements and [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) enforced by powerful institutions, typically through the threat of state _coercion_{: .warn}.
