For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), social order was maintained through *organic, bottom-up* [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) rooted in our [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) biological instincts for [reciprocity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reciprocity) and fairness within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), the scaling of massive, anonymous societies required codified systems, turning laws into *top-down inventions*. Today, this creates a highly exploitable [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Because our ancestral brains naturally _conflate_{: .warn} tribal [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) with moral absolutes, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood)—judges, politicians, and bureaucrats—uses ritual, complex jargon, and moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to make these artificial [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) feel like *immutable laws of nature*. Consequently, laws frequently function as deeply entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) that *protect arbitrary hierarchies*, property rights, and state [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) rather than serving [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) justice. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to sever the false equivalency between what is "legal" and what is "right." By returning to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and evaluating the legal code from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can _recognize laws as malleable cultural_{: .mark} _software_{: .terminal} _rather than sacred dogma_{: .mark}, judging them strictly by their [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) ability to reduce harm and support human [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology).

**See also:** [Justice and Law]({{ "/guide/human-history/laws/" | relative_url }})

---

_Learning Difficulties_{: .glossary #learning-difficulties}
: Very often, modern [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) labels _weaponised_{: .warn} by the educational system to [pathologise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#pathologize) [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) human neurodiversity. Because the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) for dynamic, contextual learning through autonomous play and [mentorship]({{ "/guide/human-history/mentorship/" | relative_url }}), _forcing_{: .warn} [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) into rigid, age-segregated, sedentary [standardisation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation) creates inevitable biological friction. Instead of recognizing this as a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) created by the unnatural demands of post-[Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) institutions, this [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) variation is _labelled_{: .warn} a "difficulty" or [disorder]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#disorder) to *enforce compliance* and protect the systemic [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) of modern schooling (see ADHD, Autism, and [Dyslexia]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dyslexia)). However, applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) demands we draw a strict, [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) line between *institutional mismatch* and genuine biological impairment. There are individuals who face profound neurological challenges—such as being non-verbal, experiencing severe cognitive delays, or lacking the physiological capacity for basic autonomy. To separate [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) diversity from *true disability*, we return to [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) and apply a critical test: Is this struggle an [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) failure of the organism's [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) **across all environments**, or is it merely friction with an artificial, culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) environment? A child who cannot process basic physical safety faces an empirical reality requiring compassionate, [harm-reduction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#harm-reduction) support; a child who simply cannot sit still and focus on abstract algebra for six hours a day is displaying a perfectly [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural) response to an _unnatural cage_{: .bold}. By wiping the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), we _stop pathologizing healthy neurodiversity while ensuring those with genuine vulnerabilities receive empirical care_{: .mark} rather than moralising [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion).

**See Also:** [Learning Difficulties]({{ "/guide/human-history/learning-difficulties/" | relative_url }})

---

_Legal Custody / Dependency_{: .glossary #legal-custody-dependency}
: A modern, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) legal fiction that formerly bound women and still legally binds a developing human exclusively to one or two adults (or the State), effectively treating them as *passive, private property* rather than autonomous individuals. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), children were raised within the *distributed, interdependent network* of the ancestral village. This provided profound [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) redundancy and afforded young people a high degree of [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) and _integration into the wider community_{: .mark}. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the need to regulate inherited wealth, land, and labour transformed familial relationships into *strict property rights*. While historical movements have largely [emancipated]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/emancipation/" | relative_url }}) [women]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-women) and marginalized adults from this status of [legal dependency]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#legal-custody-dependency), older [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) and adolescents remain _trapped_{: .warn} within this deeply entrenched [lock-in]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). This [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}) completely *overrides* a young person's biological drive for independence. By isolating [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) under the absolute [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) of their [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership), modern [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) dismantles the shared responsibility of [the tribe]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}), creating a *hyper-fragile environment* that results in chronic parental [burnout]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#burnout) and immense developmental friction. Applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) demands that we wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and view this not as a [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) law of human development, but as a historically [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _mechanism of control that actively harms human_{: .mark} [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology). (See [Adolescence]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#adolescence), [Prolonged Dependency]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#prolonged-dependency)) and [Slavery]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#slavery)  

---

_Liberty and Freedom_{: .glossary #liberty}
: The state of being free within [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) from [oppressive restrictions]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#oppression) imposed by [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) on one's way of life, [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), or political views. For roughly 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [liberty]({{ "/case-studies/law/pragmatic/liberty/" | relative_url }}) was not an *abstract political philosophy* or a legally granted privilege; it was a _biological baseline_{: .bold}. [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) and earlier humans [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) in fiercely [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands that *actively suppressed coercive dominance*, ensuring high personal [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) as a mechanism for group [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), the rise of massive, stratified societies replaced this [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) freedom with systemic [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) and *rigid* [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). Today, _the systemic restriction of human autonomy_{: .mark} is a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). To mask this reality, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and coercive institutions use manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to convince us that our *highly controlled*, culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) existence is the pinnacle of freedom. They effectively redefine [liberty]({{ "/case-studies/law/pragmatic/liberty/" | relative_url }}) as a _conditional allowance_{: .bold} granted by deeply entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins), rather than an *innate biological requirement*. Reclaiming true [liberty]({{ "/case-studies/law/pragmatic/liberty/" | relative_url }}) requires intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), we can evaluate societal structures from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), recognizing that true [freedom]({{ "/case-studies/law/pragmatic/liberty/" | relative_url }}) is not an intersubjective fiction, but an [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) *necessity for human flourishing* and the reduction of physiological [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction).

**See also:** [Liberty]({{ "/case-studies/law/pragmatic/liberty/" | relative_url }})

---

_Life-History Strategy_{: .glossary #life-history-strategy}
: The [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) blueprint dictating how an organism allocates energy to *growth, reproduction, and* [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). For humans, this strategy involves an *exceptionally long, vulnerable childhood* defined by neural plasticity and deep, comprehensive learning—a _biological gamble_{: .bold} that only succeeds when supported by the *robust*, _distributed care_{: .cool} of a multi-generational [clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan), NOT an [isolated nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) and "moderated" learning.

---

_Lobbying_{: .glossary #lobbying}
: The *systemic, legalised mechanism* by which elite [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) and massive corporations use *concentrated wealth* to protect their structural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). It completely bypasses the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) and [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [harm reduction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#harm-reduction), effectively _purchasing_{: .warn} political [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) to [exploit]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) for profit, ensuring that unevidenced, harmful systems remain **legally enforced**.

**See also:** [Lobbying]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/lobbying/" | relative_url }})

---

_Logical Fallacies_{: .glossary #logical-fallacies}
: _Glitches_{: .warn} in our [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) programming when applied to modern contexts. Many fallacies—such as appeals to [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority), the bandwagon effect, or [confirmation bias]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }})—were actually *highly adaptive* [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics) for quick [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) decisions in the Pleistocene. In the vastly more complex landscape of modern civilization, these ancient mental shortcuts become **systemic vulnerabilities** that lead to *deeply irrational conclusions*.

**See page:** [logical Fallacies]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/logical-fallacies/" | relative_url }})

---

_Majorities_{: .glossary #majorities}
: A demographic or political [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) *frequently mistaken for* [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth or moral [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority). In an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) worldview, a majority is merely a _measure_{: .bold} of [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [agreement]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus), *often driven by* [herd]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-herd) [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) and manufactured [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}). Biological reality and scientific [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}) are **immune** to [popular vote]({{ "/case-studies/politics/democracy/boundary-of-the-ballot/" | relative_url }}); a majority can easily _enforce_{: .warn} profound [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) and systemic [injustice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#justice-and-injustice) if untethered from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

**See Also:** [Minorities]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#minorities)

---

_Mammalian Care Blueprint_{: .glossary #mammalian-care-blueprint}
: The *ancient, non-negotiable* [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) requirements for raising healthy offspring, characterised by *continuous physical contact*, responsive feeding, multi-generational [alloparenting]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#alloparenting), and deep emotional [co-regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation). Modern post-Agricultural practices—such as [isolated nuclear families]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}), [age-segregation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#age-segregation), and premature separation for industrial labour—routinely _violate_{: .warn} this blueprint, resulting in severe social [deprivation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#deprivation) and **systemic psychological distress**.

**See also:** [Mammalian Care Blueprint]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/mammalian-care-blueprint/" | relative_url }})

---

_Manipulation_{: .glossary #manipulation}
: The psychological [exploitation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) of human [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) [biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) (such as guilt, trust, or the profound need for social belonging) to *control an individual's* [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) for the manipulator's benefit. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), these emotional reflexes were *essential* [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) tools; feeling guilt or a desperate need to fit in ensured *tight* [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) within the ancestral [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) and the destruction of the interdependent village, this innate trusting nature became a *highly vulnerable* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Today, manipulation frequently occurs within artificially isolated, hierarchical systems _where the victim has nowhere else to go_{: .mark}. The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and coercive authorities actively _hijack_{: .warn} these hardwired biological [fears]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear), using moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to **manufacture compliance**. They convince us that our *submission* to unnatural societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) is actually a *moral duty*, branding it as socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}). Neutralizing manipulation requires true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) and returning to a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), we can evaluate relationships and institutions from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}). This allows us to separate [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) reality from _weaponised_{: .warn} guilt, recognizing when our biologically [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _instincts for connection_{: .cool} are being [empirically]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) *used against us*.

---

_Manufactured Consensus_{: .glossary #manufactured-consensus}
: The *artificial creation* of [widespread agreement]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) within a population, not through [objective evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) or [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}), but through the *relentless repetition* of [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}). It relies on the human [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) drive for social [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) to make *culturally constructed ideologies* appear as _unquestionable_{: .warn}, **natural truths**.

---

_Marginalised Groups_{: .glossary #marginalised-groups}
: The *Vulnerable, Powerless, and Voiceless*: Individuals or groups marginalized by **hierarchical social structures** and top-down legal systems. Throughout [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), constructed laws have disproportionately _targeted_{: .warn} and *punished* these demographics to protect the interests of those with [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}) and capital.

---

_Market Forces_{: .glossary #market-forces}
: The [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) dynamics of supply, demand, and *capital allocation*. Frequently treated by modern economists and politicians *as if they were objective laws of physics*, they are actually _malleable_{: .bold} [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) systems that can—and frequently do—prioritize **infinite corporate profit** over [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) human needs, biological reality and harm to the environment.

---

_Martyrdom_{: .glossary #martyrdom}
: The *culturally celebrated act* of suffering or dying for an abstract [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) belief, [religion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion), or nation. From an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) perspective, it represents the *ultimate* [exploitation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) of [human altruism]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#altruism), _redirecting_{: .warn} the instinct to protect one's kin toward protecting an **unevidenced ideology** or [hierarchy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies).

---

_Mass Media_{: .glossary #mass-media}
: The **industrialized system** of communication that broadcasts [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}), alongside manufactured [misinformation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#misinformation), cultural [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}), and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to large populations. For most of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), we relied entirely on *locally verifiable*, face-to-face information sharing within small foraging bands. Our brains [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to *implicitly trust* the consensus of our immediate [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group), as rejecting [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) shared reality meant *isolation and death*. Today, [mass media]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/mass-media/" | relative_url }}) heavily _exploits_{: .warn} this biological vulnerability, creating a *catastrophic* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). It replaces the organic, [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) reality of the village with a *top-down megaphone* for the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and powerful institutions. By using sophisticated emotional [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and _hijacking_{: .warn} our innate [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear), they present entirely artificial societal [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) and political [agendas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agendas) as absolute, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth, _conditioning_{: .warn} us to accept profoundly [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) conditions as socially [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}). Defeating the **hypnotic influence** of [mass media]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/mass-media/" | relative_url }}) requires radical intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By applying a rigorous [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) and forcing a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), we must _treat all broadcasted claims not as trusted tribal knowledge_{: .mark}, but as highly motivated fictions that must be *ruthlessly tested* against physical [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}).

**See also:** [Mass-Media Lock-In]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/mass-media/" | relative_url }})

---

_Master/Slave Morality_{: .glossary #masterslave-morality}
: A *philosophical concept* from Friedrich Nietzsche describing two distinct moral mindsets: "Master Morality" is _pro-active_{: .bold} and values strength, nobility, and excellence; "Slave Morality" is _reactive_{: .bold} against the powerful, valuing empathy, humility, and equality as a **defensive coping mechanism**.

---

_Maturity and Seriousness_{: .glossary #maturity-and-seriousness}
: Also know as **Airs and Graces**: Highly *artificial, culturally constructed concepts* used to _enforce_{: .bold} compliance in modern societies. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), adult [Homo sapiens]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) and earlier humans relied on *lifelong play, curiosity, and flexible exploration* as deeply [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) _biological mechanisms_{: .cool} for learning, innovation, and *social bonding* within [the tribe]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), massive, stratified societies required **predictable, obedient labour**. The [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) human drive for joyful [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) became an *obstacle to economic productivity*, creating a *profound* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). To manage this, institutions and the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) _weaponised_{: .warn} "maturity". They use *manipulated* [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to [shame]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shame) adults out of their [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) playfulness, aggressively branding joy and flexibility as *"childish"*, while framing _rigid drudgery_{: .warn} as culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}). Furthermore, "seriousness" is frequently deployed as a **theatrical shield**. Bureaucrats, politicians, and managers put on airs and graces, _adopting a posture of artificial gravity_{: .mark} to _hoodwink_{: .bold} [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) into believing they are superior, "mature" decision-makers. This _performance_{: .bold} actively *discourages questioning* and protects their deeply [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) from scrutiny. True intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to *pierce this intersubjective theater*. By wiping the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and evaluating human needs from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we realise that "seriousness" is largely a *tool of* [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion), and that *lifelong play and fun is not an immature luxury*, but an [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _requirement for a healthy human biology_{: .mark}.

---

_Meaning_{: .glossary #meaning}
: An [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) *psychological and physiological state*—not a mystical aura—traditionally achieved through _direct, tangible contribution_{: .bold} to the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) and [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing) of a *close-knit* [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). For 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) rewarded us with *profound* _neurochemical satisfaction_{: .cool} for actions that kept our tribe alive, making "meaning" an undeniable, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) daily reality rooted in _deep_{: .cool} [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation). Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the rise of massive, anonymous societies _severed_{: .warn} this *direct link* between our daily labour and the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of our loved ones. Today, this creates a *devastating* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). We are _trapped_{: .bold} in atomized existences, expending our energy on **bureaucratic busywork** that benefits *distant* [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) rather than our community. Because human [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) *literally cannot thrive* without this _sense of purpose_{: .cool}, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) and corporate institutions actively _exploit_{: .warn} our starvation. They use *manipulative* [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to sell us _artificial substitutes_{: .bold}: endless consumerism, rabid political [tribalism]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}), or *blind adherence* to intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and *unevidenced* [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma). These substitutes act as **junk food for the mind**, keeping us *economically compliant* within societal [lock-ins]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#lock-ins) while leaving us _chronically unfulfilled_{: .bold}. Achieving true [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview). By wiping the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and analyzing our needs from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can _reject_{: .bold} these culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) *illusions of success* and intentionally rebuild [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) connections that _genuinely satisfy our hardwired biological drive to matter to others_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Meaning]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/meaning/" | relative_url }})

---

_Meaningful Inquiry_{: .glossary}
: A *deliberate, structured process* of questioning and investigation that is directed toward uncovering [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _truths_{: .bold} about the world. To be genuinely meaningful, the inquiry must deal with concepts that are [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) and _falsifiable_{: .cool}, moving beyond **superficial assumptions** or purely speculative "what-if" scenarios to generate *real, verifiable knowledge*.

---

_Measurable Outcomes_{: .glossary #measurable-outcomes}
: [Objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), *empirically verifiable results* used to determine if a system, policy, or law actually _achieves_{: .bold} its intended goal (e.g., reducing harm or rehabilitating, etc), standing in **stark contrast** to policies driven by [moral panic]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/moral-panics/" | relative_url }}) or [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma).

---

_Media Bias_{: .glossary #media-bias}
: The *deliberate or systemic* [manipulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#manipulation) of information to [exploit]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) human [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics)—particularly [tribalism]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}), [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear), and the [availability bias]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}). Driven by the modern **attention economy** and institutional [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}), the [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media) _prioritises_{: .bold} emotionally charged [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and intersubjective [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) over [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}), effectively manufacturing [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) and _suppressing_{: .warn} [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Medicalisation_{: .glossary #medicalisation}
: The process by which [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) human experiences, emotional distress, or profound [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) are *redefined as biological diseases* or individual pathologies requiring clinical intervention. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), physical restlessness, profound grief, or deep anxiety were *highly functional* _biological alarms_{: .cool}—signals that an organism was isolated, threatened, or living in an unsuitable environment. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), but mostly following the industrial revolution, _society engineered highly unnatural environments_{: .mark}: sedentary schooling, [isolated nuclear families]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}), and relentless, atomized wage labour. Naturally, human [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) **rebels against this friction**. Instead of acknowledging this *structural failure*, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) of modern institutions _weaponises_{: .warn} clinical [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing). They use [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) diagnostic labels to *individualise the problem*, convincing people that their perfectly healthy biological alarm system is actually a *"broken"* [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}). This deeply insidious [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) serves to treat the [symptoms]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/treating-symptoms/" | relative_url }}) of a poorly designed, culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) while aggressively _protecting_{: .bold} the [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) that caused the distress in the first place. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining distress from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can distinguish between a genuine [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) pathology and a perfectly [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) biological response to a **toxic cage**.

**See Also:** [Medicalisation]({{ "/case-studies/pseudoscience/medicalisation/" | relative_url }})

---

_Mental Health_{: .glossary #mental-health}
: The [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) *biological baseline* of psychological and emotional [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing) that occurs when a human organism operates within an environment _suited_{: .cool} to its [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) hardware. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), mental health was *organically regulated* through deep, interdependent [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), physical movement, and undeniable [meaning]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#meaning) within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), the rise of atomized, highly coercive societies created a **devastating** [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Today, what institutions frequently diagnose as a *"mental health crisis"* is actually the perfectly [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) functioning of our biological alarm systems, reacting to a severe _lack_{: .warn} of [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) and community. To avoid acknowledging this *structural failure*, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) heavily relies on clinical [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and intersubjective [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to _individualize_{: .warn} the problem. They subtly redefine "mental health" _merely as a person's ability to silently cope with_{: .mark}, and economically produce within, profoundly [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). True intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining human needs from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can stop [medicalising]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#medicalisation) our distress and recognise that genuine mental [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing) requires [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) *changes to our environment*, not just **endless adaptation** to a culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) but biologically *toxic cage*.

**See also:** [Mental Health, Human History Section]({{ "/guide/human-history/mental-health/" | relative_url }})

**See also:** [Mental Health, Health Series]({{ "/case-studies/health/mental-health/" | relative_url }})

---

_Mental Shortcuts_{: .glossary #mental-shortcuts}
: Also known as [heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics); the *rapid, unconscious* decision-making processes _hardwired_{: .bold} into our brains by **millions of years** of [natural selection]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}). Optimized for immediate physical [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) in the ancestral environment rather than [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth-seeking, these shortcuts frequently _misfire_{: .warn} as [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) in the complex, artificial environments of modern [civilisation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#civilisations), making humans **highly vulnerable** to systemic [manipulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#manipulation).

---

_Mentorship_{: .glossary #mentorship}
: The deeply biological, [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) *transmission of knowledge*, skills, and emotional regulation from an experienced individual to an autonomous learner. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), learning did not occur in _coercive_{: .warn}, age-segregated factories; it occurred through _voluntary_{: .cool}, contextual mentorship within the interdependent [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). The ancestral brain [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to *absorb information dynamically* through play, observation, and [reciprocal]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reciprocity) social trust, driven by the learner's own hardwired curiosity. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), this horizontally integrated system was replaced by a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) of modern schooling instituted **rigid, top-down** [standardised education]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/education/" | relative_url }}), using manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to _convince society_{: .bold} that learning requires strict [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion), constant testing, and the complete [suppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression) of a child's [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy). This artificial, culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _paradigm_{: .bold} creates immense **biological friction**, *pathologising those who naturally resist it* (see [Learning Difficulties]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#learning-difficulties)). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining human development from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can dismantle these [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) and recognise that true mastery is an [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) process that _requires mutual respect and self-directed exploration_{: .mark}, not institutional force.

---

_Meritocracy_{: .glossary #meritocracy}
: An [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) cultural [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) claiming that [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}), wealth, and [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}) are *distributed purely according to individual talent* and effort. From an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) perspective, it frequently functions as a [comforting myth]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) used to _legitimise_{: .warn} systemic [inequality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inequality), **actively ignoring** the massive advantages of _inherited capital_{: .bold}, epigenetic history, and structural [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}).

---

_Mindfulness_{: .glossary #mindfulness}
: A psychological and meditative practice often _co-opted_{: .warn} by modern institutions and corporate systems to *help individuals cope* with highly unnatural environments. While potentially useful for personal stress reduction, it is frequently deployed as a tool to **adapt human beings** to systemic [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) rather than *addressing the root causes* of their _alienation_{: .bold}.

---

_Minorities_{: .glossary #minorities}
: Groups within a [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) that *lack dominant social, political, or economic* [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}). Under unevidenced or **tribally biased** legal systems, [minorities]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/majorities-vs-minorities/" | relative_url }}) frequently face systemic [injustice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#justice-and-injustice), [scapegoating]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scapegoats), and _disproportionate punishment_{: .warn}.

---

_Misinformation_{: .glossary #misinformation}
: *False, inaccurate, or unevidenced information* that spreads rapidly within a population, often _masquerading_{: .warn} as established fact. For over 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) depended on _absolute trust_{: .bold} within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group); the brain [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to **prioritize tribal consensus**, social cohesion, and emotionally charged [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) over cold, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) accuracy. Because rejecting [the tribe's]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) shared reality historically meant **deadly isolation**, we are *biologically wired to believe* what our peers believe. Today, within the massive, hyper-connected societies that followed the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), this hardwired vulnerability is a _catastrophic_{: .warn} [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood), algorithmic media, and [ideological]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) [True Believers]({{ "/guide/obstacles/true-believers/" | relative_url }}) actively exploit this biological blind spot. They use fear-based [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to **bypass our critical faculties**, propagating unevidenced [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) to protect their [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}). Because sharing these fictions _functions as a signal of tribal loyalty_{: .mark} rather than a quest for truth, [misinformation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#misinformation) thrives effortlessly within artificially constructed [echo chambers]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#echo-chambers), _calcifying_{: .bold} into oppressive [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). Defeating this requires radical intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation). By actively adopting a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) and forcing a [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate), we must *consciously override* our [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) urge to conform, evaluating all claims strictly from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) and insisting upon [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical), [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}).

_Mistreatment_{: .glossary #mistreatment}
: The **harmful**, **unjust**, or **abusive** treatment of an individual. In a [sociological]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/" | relative_url }}) context, the isolated, private, and high-stress environment of the modern [nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) frequently _enables_{: .warn} mistreatment to occur [behind closed doors]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#behind-closed-doors), stripped of the protective, watchful eyes of a [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), extended *tribal community*.

**See Also:** [Neglect and Abuse]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/neglect-and-abuse/" | relative_url }})

**See Also:** [Abuse]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#abuse)

_Modern Humans / Homo sapiens_{: .glossary #modern-humans-homo-sapiens}
: The _specific biological species to which all living human beings belong_{: .mark}, originating r**oughly 300,000 years ago**, characterized by a *unique capacity* for abstract thought, complex language, and symbolic [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture). For over 99.5% of our existence, _Homo sapiens_{: .bold} and earlier humans [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) in *highly* [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands; our biological hardware is strictly *adapted* for _close-knit_{: .cool} [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), physical _movement_{: .cool}, lifelong _play_{: .cool}, and high personal [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) within an intimate [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group). Our unique capacity for complex language allowed us to invent [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }})—an evolutionary _superpower_{: .bold} that enabled us to scale cooperation globally. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), we used this _software_{: .terminal} to build massive, stratified societies that fundamentally _contradict_{: .warn} our _ancient hardware_{: .termnal}. We are essentially _Pleistocene_{: .bold} apes living in a highly industrialized, culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) cage, resulting in a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). To maintain this *artificial order*, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) weaponizes our hardwired [tribal instincts]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}), using moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to *convince us* that systemic [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion), relentless wage labour, and isolation are somehow [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) human traits. Achieving true [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a rigorous [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to _sever our identity from these invented stories_{: .mark}. By wiping the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and looking at our species from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can stop [pathologising]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#pathologize) ourselves for failing to thrive in [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) societal [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}), and instead demand environments that actually serve the [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) *biological needs* of _Homo sapiens_{: .bold}.

_Monogamy_{: .glossary #monogamy}
: A *mating system* where an individual has **only one partner**. While serial monogamy or flexible pair bonding occurs naturally in humans, the rigid, legally enforced, and lifelong exclusivity _demanded_{: .warn} by modern cultural and [religious]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion) institutions is a **social construct** largely designed to _control_{: .warn} reproduction, property, and [patrilineal]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#patriarch-and-patrilineal) inheritance following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}).

_Moral Absolutes_{: .glossary #moral-absolutes}
: Rigid, culturally constructed [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) _dictating_{: .warn} what is unconditionally **"good"** or **"evil"**. These [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) concepts *frequently ignore* [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) context, biological _nuance_{: .cool}, and [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [harm reduction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#harm-reduction), serving instead to enforce [dogmatic]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) compliance.

_Moral Failure_{: .glossary #moral-failure}
: A *culturally constructed label* used by [The Herd]({{ "/guide/" | relative_url }}) or institutional authorities to [blame]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/blame/" | relative_url }}) and [shame]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shame) individuals who fail to [conform]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) to _arbitrary_{: .warn} social [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}). From an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) perspective, what is frequently condemned as an individual's **"moral failure"** (such as parental [burnout]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#burnout) or addiction) is actually *a predictable biological response* to a systemic [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}).

_Moralisation_{: .glossary #moralisation}
: The [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) process of attaching arbitrary **"good"** or **"evil"** labels to [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) human behaviours. This [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) mechanism is _frequently weaponised by institutions and religions_{: .mark} to enforce [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience), [shame]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shame) [diversity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#diversity), and protect the [status quo]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#status-quo).

_Moral Outrage_{: .glossary #moral-outrage}
: An *intense emotional reaction* of anger or disgust triggered by a _perceived_{: .warn} "violation" of **moral boundaries**, often used socially to signal tribal loyalty or police group behaviour.

**See Also:** [Moral Outrage]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/moral-outrage/" | relative_url }})

_Moral Panic_{: .glossary #moral-panic}
: A widespread, _exaggerated_{: .warn} [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) or *mass anxiety* within a society, typically sparked by a [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) about a specific group, _subculture_{: .bold}, or _behaviour_{: .bold} _perceived as a threat to moral order_{: .mark}.

**See Also:** [Moral Panics]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/moral-panics/" | relative_url }})

_Motivated Reasoning_{: .glossary #motivated-reasoning}
: The *unconscious psychological process* of **bending logic** and filtering [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) to protect one's _pre-existing_{: .bold} [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), tribal identity, or social [status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }}). Originating as an [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) _defense mechanism_{: .cool} designed to prevent the deep biological terror of [out-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}) exile, it causes [modern humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#modern-humans-homo-sapiens) to _aggressively_{: .warn} defend [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [social constructions]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) and reject [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) reality simply to *maintain harmony* with [The Herd]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-herd).

_Myth_{: .glossary #myth}
: A [traditional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#tradition) story, [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or widely held but false [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), often used by cultures to *explain the unexplainable* or cement social [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) before _scientific explanations_{: .neon} existed.

_Myth vs. Myth_{: .glossary #myth-vs-myth}
: The **devastating** historical and modern phenomenon where _competing_{: .warn} [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) collide. Because humans [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to form _tight-knit_{: .cool} [tribes]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) bonded by shared [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), post-Agricultural societies scale this instinct up, replacing kin with **untestable ideologies** (religions, nationalisms, or political dogmas). When these fictions clash, the biological drive to protect the "tribe" is weaponised, causing [True Believers]({{ "/guide/obstacles/true-believers/" | relative_url }}) to **enact horrific violence**, [oppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#oppression), and [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) to defend essentially unevidenced, [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [illusions]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#illusion).

**See Also:** [Myth vs. Myth]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/myth-vs-myth/" | relative_url }})

_Nationalism_{: .glossary #nationalism}
: An [ideology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) and _identification_{: .bold} with one's own nation, often promoting its interests above those of all other nations, driven by an [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) [in-group/out-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}) **tribal mentality**.

_Nation State_{: .glossary #nation-state}
: A sovereign state whose citizens are relatively _homogeneous_{: .bold} in factors such as language or common descent; a modern, entirely [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) construct used to organize large populations. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), our biological [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) was _limited to roughly 150 face-to-face relationships_{: .mark} within a foraging band. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), scaling [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) among millions of anonymous strangers required a massive evolutionary _hack_{: .terminal}. The [Nation State]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#nation-state) is this ultimate **"imagined community"**. To *make it work*, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) of politicians, educators, and the military must aggressively _hijack_{: .warn} our ancient tribal instincts. They use flags, anthems, manipulated [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing), and historical [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to **trick the primitive** [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) into feeling kin-level _loyalty_{: .cool} toward _invisible lines on a map_{: .mark}. While effective as _administrative software_{: .termnal}, this creates a deeply dangerous [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). When [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) treats these historically [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), artificial boundaries as unquestionable, "natural" laws, they _calcify_{: .bold} into _oppressive_{: .warn} [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). Ideological [True Believers]({{ "/guide/obstacles/true-believers/" | relative_url }}) enforce *blind submission* to this [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma), convincing individuals to sacrifice their lives and [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) for what is essentially an administrative zone. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview). By wiping the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and analyzing the world from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can _demystify_{: .bold} the [Nation State]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#nation-state), recognising that borders are not [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) realities of physics, but *highly malleable* _social software_{: .terminal} _that we have the right to rewrite_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Nation-State]({{ "/case-studies/politics/miscelleneous/nation-state/" | relative_url }})

_Natural_{: .glossary #natural}
: Traits, behaviours, or instincts that [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) biologically over deep [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) time. Crucially *distinct* from **"normal"**, [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) refers _strictly to what is hardwired into human_{: .mark} [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) to aid [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival), *regardless* of whether a specific [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture) currently _accepts_{: .cool} or _condemns_{: .warn} it.

_Natural Selection_{: .glossary #natural-selection}
: The *blind*, [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _evolutionary process_{: .bold} by which _traits that enhance an organism's ability to survive and reproduce become more common over successive generations_{: .mark}. For _Homo sapiens_{: .bold} and earlier humans, this [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) biological mechanism operated for **hundreds of thousands of years**, *perfectly tuning* our _physical_{: .cool} and _psychological_{: .cool} _hardware_{: .terminal} for life in small, highly [cooperative]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands. Because [natural selection]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural-selection) is a **painfully slow**, intergenerational process, it cannot keep pace with the *rapid explosion* of human [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture). Consequently, the _radical environmental changes_{: .mark} introduced after the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }})—which occurred in a mere evolutionary blink—have left us with a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Our bodies and brains are _hardwired_{: .terminal} for a [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) environment that *no longer exists*. To mask the systemic failures of modern life, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) often weaponizes this biological reality through malicious [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) (such as "Social Darwinism"), falsely _claiming_{: .warn} that **cutthroat capitalist competition** and arbitrary [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) are [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of the fittest. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By *understanding* _natural selection_{: .bold} from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can demystify these [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}), recognising [evolution]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) not as a **moral justification** for modern _exploitation_{: .warn}, but as the strict _engineering blueprint_{: .terminal} of our _biological needs_{: .cool}—proving exactly why our culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) often _feels so physically and emotionally unlivable_{: .mark}.

_Neglect_{: .glossary #neglect}
: The **systemic** or **interpersonal failure** to provide for an individual's [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) developmental, emotional, or biological needs. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) were raised within the rich, distributed network of a foraging band. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the *artificial isolation* of the [nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) created a devastating [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Crucially, even in the *vast majority* of homes where [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) are deeply loving, attentive, and well-intentioned, this [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [lock-in]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) _inevitably enforces a subtle but profound form of systemic neglect_{: .mark}. Because it is an [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) impossibility for one or two adults to *replace an entire tribe*, [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) are structurally _deprived_{: .warn} of the *evolutionary necessities* of a wider _community_{: .cool}: *multiple* secure _attachment figures_{: .cool}, diverse horizontal [mentorship]({{ "/guide/human-history/mentorship/" | relative_url }}), age-mixed _autonomous play_{: .cool}, and the deep _psychological resilience_{: .cool} born from *social redundancy*. Furthermore, because [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) are _trapped_{: .warn} within this isolated familial bubble and then processed through [standardized]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation) institutions, they _lack the organic friction of diverse, community-wide perspectives_{: .mark}. As a result, they *routinely emerge* into adulthood **heavily indoctrinated** by the specific [religious]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion) [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) or secular [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) of their [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) and the state, stripped of their [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and *largely unable* to genuinely [think for themselves]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires us to **stop blaming** individual [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) for _failing_{: .warn} at an *impossible task*, and instead apply a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to recognise from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}) that _healthy human development biologically requires a village_{: .mark}.

**See Also:** [Redefining Neglect and Abuse]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/neglect-and-abuse/" | relative_url }})
 
_Neolithic_{: .glossary}
: Meaning **"New Stone Age"**, this pivotal _archaeological_{: .bold} period marks the *final stage of technological development* among prehistoric humans before the advent of _metalworking_{: .bold}. Spanning roughly from 10,000 BCE to between 4,500 and 2,000 BCE, it is defined by the widespread adoption of polished stone tools, the [transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) from nomadic foraging to settled agricultural communities, and the initial _domestication_{: .bold} of plants and animals. This era created the *foundational societal surpluses* that allowed for **specialised labour**, permanent **property ownership**, and the eventual rise of the world's first **complex civilizations**.

_Neuroscience_{: .glossary #neuroscience}
: The [scientific study]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) of the _nervous system_{: .bold} and the _brain_{: .bold}. By revealing how the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) processes stress, learns, and _regulates emotion_{: .cool}, _neuroscience_{: .bold} provides [objective evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) that our minds were optimized for *ancestral, cooperative environments*, proving that modern institutional systems frequently _trigger_{: .warn} chronic biological [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction).

_Normal_{: .glossary #normal}
: The current, *culturally accepted standards*, [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules), or **expectations** of a specific society. Distinct from [natural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#natural), what is considered [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) is simply a [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) byproduct of very recent [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), frequently [locking in]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) harmful [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) and demanding unquestioning [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity).

_Normalisation_{: .glossary #normalisation}
: The [sociological]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/" | relative_url }}) process by which an _unnatural_{: .warn}, culturally constructed [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), or system is **repeatedly enforced** until it becomes accepted as the standard "[normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }})". This *process* relies heavily on human [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) and [in-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}) [biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) to blind populations to the [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) harms or [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) of a given practice. Or, conversely, that *thoughts* or *behaviours* deemed **"abnormal"**, are in fact perfectly [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) (which can also lead to great harm and internal suffering) such as play and [sexuality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sexuality).

_Norms_{: .glossary #norms}
: The *unwritten behavioral expectations* and social [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) that govern how individuals interact within a specific [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture) or group. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) emerged organically from the bottom up; in [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands, these [unspoken rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) directly _facilitated_{: .cool} tight [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) and [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) within the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group), remaining perfectly aligned with our _biological hardware_{: .terminal}. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#agricultural-transition), the scale of [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) *shifted drastically*. Today, in massive, stratified societies, what we call [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) are **rarely organic**. They are highly engineered, top-down [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) constructs designed to enforce compliance and protect entrenched [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). Because our [ancestral brains]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) are _hardwired_{: .bold} to dread **social rejection**—which historically meant isolation and death—_we are deeply vulnerable to this control_{: .mark}. The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) actively exploits this ancient [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) mechanism, using moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to brand deeply unnatural behaviours—such as relentless economic productivity, *artificial* _"professionalism"_{: .bold}, and sedentary compliance—as polite, proper, and culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}). This creates a chronic [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}), as individuals **quietly suffer** to uphold societal [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) that actively harm their [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a rigorous [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining **social expectations** from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can demystify these [unwritten rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules), separating [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _necessities_{: .cool} from _manipulable cultural fictions_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }})

_Nuclear Family_{: .glossary #nuclear-family}
: A **socially constructed** [family unit]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) consisting solely of one or two [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) and their [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children), living in **private isolation**. While heavily _idealised_{: .bold} by modern cultural and legal [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), it is a *profound* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). For **millions of years**, human [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) were *raised collectively* by extended kin and [tribal members]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}); the *nuclear model* forces **unnatural burdens** on [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership) (often single [parents]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#parental-ownership)) and _deprives_{: .warn} [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) (who have been made **"property"** in legal **"custody"**) of diverse [role models]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-models) and multiple [mentorship]({{ "/guide/human-history/mentorship/" | relative_url }}).

**See also:** [Nuclear Family]({{ "/case-studies/health/family/" | relative_url }})

_Nutrition_{: .glossary #nutrition}
: The [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) *biological requirement* for the specific fuels and building blocks the human body [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) to consume. The modern **industrial food system** represents a severe [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}), replacing _nutrient-dense_{: .bold}, [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) diets with highly _processed_{: .warn}, hyper-palatable products engineered to [exploit]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) our [evolved cravings]({{ "/case-studies/health/diet/" | relative_url }}) for sugar, salt, and fat. These cravings [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) as sugar, salt, and fat were _rare for millions of years_{: .mark}: until now, when they are **massively overconsumed**.

**See Also:** [Diet]({{ "/case-studies/health/diet/" | relative_url }})

_Obedience_{: .glossary #obedience}
: Uncritical _compliance_{: .bold} with the **orders**, [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules), or [dogmas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma) of an [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) figure.
