Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By _assessing_{: .bold} **societal failures** strictly from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can *bypass* these **manipulative fictions**, demanding [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _accountability_{: .bold} for systemic [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) _rather than blindly turning our hardwired aggression against the powerless_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Scapegoats]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/scapegoats/" | relative_url }}) and [Blame]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/blame/" | relative_url }})

_Sciency-Sounding_{: .glossary #sciency-sounding}
: A _deceptive_{: .warn} **rhetorical style** that *borrows* the _technical terminology_{: .bold} of _science_{: .neon}—using _jargon_{: .warn} like "vibrations", "energy fields", or "quantum"—to _mimic the appearance of authority_{: .mark}. Despite the *advanced language*, these concepts _fail_{: .warn} to provide [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) hypotheses or stand up to real [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) scrutiny, functioning instead as a _mask_{: .bold} for **unproven** or [pseudoscientific]({{ "/case-studies/pseudoscience/" | relative_url }}) ideas.

_Secrecy_{: .glossary #secrecy}
: A _defencive_{: .bold} behavioral response _forced_{: .warn} upon individuals when [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _human expressions are heavily moralised, stigmatized, or criminalized by an oppressive, unevidenced society_{: .mark}. This **secrecy** (*especially if exposed*) is then [framed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) as a **"psychological pathology"** or inherent moral [deviance]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#deviant). By [labeling]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#labeling) the individual as **"untrustworthy"** or **"manipulative"**, the dominant [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture) successfully [blames]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/blame/" | relative_url }}) the victim, distracting from the unnatural, [coercive]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) _that made honest expression unsafe in the first place_{: .mark}.

_Self-Fulfilling Prophecies_{: .glossary #self-fulfilling-prophecies}
: A [psychological]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) and [sociological]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/" | relative_url }}) phenomenon where a **false belief**, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [label]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#labeling), or [cultural]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture) _expectation_{: .bold} _directly influences people's behaviour in a way that causes the original false premise to actually come true_{: .mark}. This _mechanism_{: .bold} is *frequently* [exploited]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) by [institutions]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/institutional-control/" | relative_url }}) to *validate unevidenced* [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and **control** [out-groups]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}).

_Self-Sacrifice_{: .glossary #self-sacrifice}
: _Relinquishing_{: .bold} personal resources, safety, or reproductive advantage for the _benefit_{: .cool} of [the group]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}). In evolutionary [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology), this trait ensured the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of kin networks and the wider tribe. In absolute genetic terms, _sacrificing oneself to protect close relatives ensures that shared DNA continues to propagate within the broader gene pool_{: .mark}; through the lens of [inclusive fitness]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inclusive-fitness-kin-selection), what appears as a profound individual loss is actually a *mathematical victory* for the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) and _continuation_{: .bold} of those *shared genes*.

**See also:** [Self-Sacrifice]({{ "/guide/human-history/self-sacrifice/" | relative_url }})

_Sense of Doom_{: .glossary #sense-of-doom}
: A chronic, systemic **psychological distress** caused by a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). While [early humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#early-humans) experienced acute [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) in response to immediate, tangible threats, modern post-Agricultural societies _subject our nervous systems to relentless, abstract, and unresolvable_{: .mark} [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _threats_{: .warn} (via the [attention economy]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/attention/" | relative_url }})), paralyzing our [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) and **manufacturing** a state of _fearful compliance_{: .bold}.

_Sexuality_{: .glossary #sexuality}
: A *deeply* _ancient_{: .bold}, _diverse_{: .bold}, and _hardwired_{: .bold} [primate inheritance]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/primate-blueprint/" | relative_url }}) that serves *vital* [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) _functions far beyond mere reproduction_{: .mark}, such as [co-regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation) and [inclusive fitness]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inclusive-fitness-kin-selection). While our immense _prefrontal cortex_{: .bold} provides our *unique human capacity* for _abstract thought_{: .cool} and _impulse control_{: .cool}, our *underlying biological* _hardware_{: .terminal} remains _fundamentally primal_{: .bold}. Much like our closest evolutionary relatives—chimpanzees and bonobos—our ancestors utilised [sexual behaviour]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sexuality) as an [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _toolkit_{: .cool} for conflict _resolution_{: .bold}, social _bonding_{: .bold}, _alliance_{: .bold} formation, [mentorship]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mentorship) and _stress reduction_{: .bold}. For **millions of years**, including **over 99%** of modern human (*Homo sapiens*) [history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) within [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands and earlier societies, these [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) behaviours were fluid and _largely devoid of the artificial concepts_{: .mark} of [shame]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shame) or **legal stigma**. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the invention of private property required strict, [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}) over _reproduction_{: .bold} to ensure the "correct" **transfer of wealth**. To enforce these new [patriarchal]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#patriarch-and-patrilineal) [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies), the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood)—aided powerfully by theological [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) such as Thomas Aquinas's *Summa Theologica*—engineered heavy [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) of guilt, [sin]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sin), and bodily [suppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression). By [legally]({{ "/case-studies/law/theory/" | relative_url }}) _criminalising_{: .warn} and morally [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) our base _primate nature_{: .bold} as **"dirty"** or **"disordered"**, [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) _manufactured_{: .warn} a *devastating* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}), _turning a neurochemical engine of social cohesion into a source of chronic anxiety and systemic_{: .mark} [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion). Achieving true intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires wiping the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and viewing [human biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}). By stripping away culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma), we can recognise our [sexuality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sexuality) _not as a moral failing to be violently policed, but as a healthy, integral component of our biological hardware_{: .mark}.

_Shame_{: .glossary #shame}
: An [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) *moral emotion* originally used in small [tribes]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) to **discourage** [freeloading]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#freeloading) and *maintain social harmony*. In modern, [post-transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) societies, it is frequently _weaponised_{: .warn} by [The Herd]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-herd) and [institutions]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/institutional-control/" | relative_url }}) _to punish nonconformity and enforce unnatural taboos_{: .mark}.

_Shared Eyes_{: .glossary #shared-eyes}
: The [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) *social dynamic* of ancestral hunter-gatherer bands, _where life was lived communally and transparently_{: .mark}. This constant, shared _visibility_{: .bold} acted as a powerful [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) safeguard against [cruelty]({{ "/guide/human-history/altruism-and-cruelty/" | relative_url }}), [exploitation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation), [mistreatment]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mistreatment), [neglect/abuse]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/neglect-and-abuse/" | relative_url }}) and/or [brainwashing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#brainwashing) of the vulnerable.

_Sin_{: .glossary #sin}
: A **culturally constructed** [religious]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion) _concept_{: .bold} used to _moralise_{: .warn} [natural human instincts]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) and enforce [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience) to _unevidenced doctrines under the threat of supernatural punishment_{: .mark}.

_Slavery_{: .glossary #slavery}
: A brutal, historically widespread **legal fiction** where human beings were _classified_{: .warn} as [property]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#legal-custody-dependency). It serves as _the ultimate proof that just because a practice is legally codified and normalized by a society does not make it_{: .mark} [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), _ethical_{: .bold}, or _scientifically_{: .neon} _justifiable_{: .bold}. In 2026, many people in the world remain "property" (see [Emancipation]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/emancipation/" | relative_url }}))

_Slogans_{: .glossary #slogans}
: _Simplified, emotionally charged phrases_{: .mark} used by the [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media), politicians, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/guide/obstacles/secular-priesthood/" | relative_url }}) and [The Herd]({{ "/guide/" | relative_url }}) to *replace* [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) and [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) _analysis_{: .cool} with [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [tribal signaling]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}) and [moral outrage]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/moral-outrage/" | relative_url }}).

_Social Capital_{: .glossary #social-capital}
: The _networks of relationships, shared values, and trust within a community that enable its members to cooperate, support one another, and function effectively_{: .mark} as [a group]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}).

_Social Construction (Cultural Construction)_{: .glossary #social-construction}
: A _concept_{: .bold}, [belief]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), or institutional [norm]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) that exists strictly because a [human society]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#anthropology) has collectively agreed to _invent_{: .warn} and _enforce_{: .warn} it, rather than being an [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) law of physics or [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology). While _social constructs_{: .bold}—such as currency, national borders, and traditional [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies)—exert immense real-world [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}), *recognising* them as mutable products of human [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture) *rather than* unchangeable [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}) is essential for [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) _analysis_{: .cool} and systemic _reform_{: .cool}.

_Social Contract_{: .glossary #social-contract}
: In modern _political theory_{: .bold}: the implicit, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [agreement]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) among members of a [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) to _cooperate for social benefits_{: .mark}, typically by sacrificing some individual [freedom]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) in return for state [protection]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#protection-and-protectors) and _order_{: .bold}.

**See also:** [The Social Contract]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/social-contract/" | relative_url }})

_Social Control_{: .glossary #social-control}
: The **exertion** of [power]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/power/" | relative_url }}) by [institutions]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/state-institutions/" | relative_url }}), [cultural narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or authorities to *regulate human* [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) and _enforce_{: .warn} **compliance** with [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}). Often *disguised* as **"benevolence"**, it relies on [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}), [peer pressure]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#peer-pressure), [and top-down rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules) to maintain the [status quo]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#status-quo).

**See also:** [Social-Control Lock-In]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/social-control/" | relative_url }})

_Social Instability_{: .glossary #social-instability}
: The systemic _breakdown_{: .warn} of _order_{: .bold}, _trust_{: .bold}, and [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) *within a population*. Often viewed merely as a political or policing failure, it is more accurately understood as the inevitable biological and psychological consequence of extreme [inequality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inequality), _alienation_{: .warn}, and [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) reaching a **breaking point**.

_Social Instincts_{: .glossary #social-instincts}
: The [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate), _hardwired drives_{: .terminal} that compel humans to seek _belonging_{: .cool}, [cooperate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), _share emotions_{: .cool}, and *read social cues*. While essential for the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of [early humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#early-humans) in the wild, these same instincts can be easily _hijacked_{: .warn} in **modern environments** by [propaganda]({{ "/guide/human-history/propaganda/" | relative_url }}), social media, and [tribal]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}) [ideologies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) to enforce [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity).

_Socialisation_{: .glossary #socialisation}
: The _lifelong process_{: .bold} by which individuals *learn and absorb* the [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}), _customs_{: .bold}, [values]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/values/" | relative_url }}), and [ideologies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) of their [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}).

**See also:** [Socialisation]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/socialisation/" | relative_url }})

_Social Learning_{: .glossary #social-learning}
: The [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), _biological method_{: .cool} by which humans acquire skills and [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) through _observation_{: .bold}, [play]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/play/" | relative_url }}), and _direct participation_{: .cool} within a [community]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}), _contrasting_{: .warn} sharply with the **artificial**, [top-down]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}) instruction of [modern education]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/education/" | relative_url }}).

_Social Structure(s)_{: .glossary #social-structures}
: The complex patterns of relationships, [institutions], and roles that organize and govern a society.

**See also:** [Social Structures]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }})

_Social Trust_{: .glossary #social-trust}
: The _inter-personal_{: .bold} and _communal_{: .cool} *glue* that allowed early human bands to **survive**. In ancestral hunter-gatherer [groups]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}), it was based on direct [reputation tracking]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reputation-tracking), [reciprocity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reciprocity), and shared [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). In modern mass societies, it relies on *fragile*, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [institutions]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/state-institutions/" | relative_url }}) and is **easily eroded** by [inequality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inequality), [urban anonymity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#urbanisation), and broken social contracts.

_Societal Reactions_{: .glossary #societal-reactions}
: The [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), often **emotionally driven responses** of a [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture) or population to a [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) or _event_{: .bold}. In an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) system, people, [institions]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/state-institutions/" | relative_url }}) and [laws]({{ "/case-studies/law/theory/" | relative_url }}) should *target direct*, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _harm_{: .warn}, _not_{: .mark} merely _appease_{: .bold} the [outrage]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/moral-outrage/" | relative_url }}), [tribalism]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}), or [moral panics]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/moral-panics/" | relative_url }}) _generated by societal reactions_{: .mark}.

_Sociology_{: .glossary #sociology}
: The _academic study_{: .cool} of _human society_{: .bold}, [social structures]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}), and cultural [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}). When *grounded* in evolutionary [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) and [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), [sociology]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/" | relative_url }}) is a *vital tool* for _analysing_{: .cool} how [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) systems, [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies), and [cultural narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) shape [human behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) and enforce [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}).

_Sound Judgement_{: .glossary #sound-judgement}
: The _deliberate, cognitive process of arriving at a conclusion based strictly on_{: .mark} [objective evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), and [testable]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) reality. It requires *actively overriding* the rapid, **emotionally driven** mental shortcuts ([heuristics]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#heuristics)) that our hunter-gatherer [brains]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) naturally rely on, _rejecting_{: .warn} the [comforting consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) of [The Herd]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-herd) in favour of rigorous, independent _analysis_{: .cool}.

_Speculation_{: .glossary}
: The _formation_{: .bold} of a theory, conjecture, or opinion without firm, [supporting evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}); *a necessary starting point* for _creative thinking_{: .cool} or _generating hypotheses_{: .cool}, but _dangerous_{: .warn} when **treated as** a verified fact.

_Standardisation_{: .glossary #standardisation}
: The [institutional drive]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/state-institutions/" | relative_url }}) to _force_{: .warn} [diverse]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#diversity) human [behaviours]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), bodies, and minds into a single, **uniform model** to maximize efficiency, predictability, and [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}). In a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview), [standardisation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation) frequently represents a fundamental _denial_{: .warn} of the [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) biological variation shaped by [evolution]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}).

_State Power_{: .glossary #state-power}
: The centralized, institutional [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) wielded by a government to enforce laws, collect taxes, and maintain order—a _hierarchical construct_{: .bold} that _emerged as a dominant force only after_{: .mark} the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}).

_Statistics_{: .glossary #statistics}
: The _mathematical science_{: .cool} of collecting, analysing, and interpreting data. While it is an essential, [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) tool of the [scientific method]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}), it is *frequently* [weaponised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) by [institutions]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/state-institutions/" | relative_url }}), politicians, and the [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media) through selective [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to [exploit]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#exploitation) [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) and manufacture [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus).

_Status_{: .glossary #status}
: An individual's *relative* social, "professional", or tribal standing within a group; an [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) _psychological drive that heavily influences access to resources and social leverage_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Status]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/status/" | relative_url }})

_Status Quo_{: .glossary #status-quo}
: The _existing state of affairs, especially regarding social, cultural, or political issues_{: .mark}.

_Stigma_{: .glossary #stigma}
: A [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) **mark of disgrace** or **severe disapproval** attached to individuals or groups who _deviate_{: .bold} from *culturally enforced* [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}). Stigma operates as a powerful mechanism of [peer pressure]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#peer-pressure) and [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}), [weaponised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) to maintain tribal [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) and punish those who exhibit [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _biological variation_{: .cool} or [independent thought]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}).

_Storm and Stress_{: .glossary #storm-and-stress}
: A [culturally constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction), [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narrative]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) used by modern [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) to _dismiss_{: .warn} the *legitimate psychological distress of teenagers*. Rather than _recognising_{: .bold} their anxiety, rebellion, or disengagement as *predictable biological reactions* to a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) (i.e., being denied [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) and purpose), [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) _incorrectly_{: .mark} [frames]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) _these behaviours as "inherent biological defects" of the_{: .mark} [teenage]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/adolescence/" | relative_url }}) [brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}).

_Strangers_{: .glossary #strangers}
: Unknown individuals outside of one's kin or trust network. For 99.5% of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), humans lived in small bands where true "strangers" were _extremely rare_{: .bold} and encountered with **high caution**. Modern [urban anonymity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#urbanisation) _forces humans to live surrounded by thousands of strangers_{: .mark}, a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}).

_Stress_{: .glossary #stress}
: An [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) _biological response_{: .bold} (the *fight-or-flight* mechanism) designed to keep [early humans]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#early-humans) alive during acute, *short-term* physical threats. In modern, [post-transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) societies, unnatural demands like consumer anxiety, artificial light, and bureaucratic busywork trigger this system _constantly_{: .warn}, turning an adaptive [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) _tool_{: .cool} _into a source of chronic, systemic damage_{: .mark}.

_Subjective Phenomena_{: .glossary #subjective-phenomena}
: Internal, individual experiences or feelings (such as the taste of food or personal emotional states) that **cannot** be _objectively measured_{: .cool} or universally verified. While biologically real to the individual experiencing them, _they cannot be used as the_{: .mark} [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _foundation for societal laws or scientific truth_{: .mark}.

_Sub-optimal Attachment_{: .glossary #sub-optimal-attachment}
: A highly [subjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [sciency-sounding]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sciency-sounding) _metric_{: .bold} _weaponised_{: .warn} by the modern [child protection]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#child-protection) bureaucracy. Because _The State cannot objectively measure love or replicate the rich, multi-generational care of a hunter-gatherer tribe_{: .mark}, it invents nebulous, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) standards of "perfect" [parenting]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#intensive-parenting). These standards are frequently used to **penalise** marginalised [families]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}) who simply lack the capital to hide their [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) distress [behind closed doors]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#behind-closed-doors).

_Surveillance and Incarceration_{: .glossary #surveillance-and-incarceration}
: Modern _institutional methods of monitoring populations and confining individuals to enforce compliance_{: .mark}. Historically and practically, these _tools_{: .bold} were designed by _elites_{: .warn} to maintain [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}) and **deter dissent**, rather than to achieve _restorative_{: .bold} [justice]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#justice-and-injustice) or [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _rehabilitation_{: .cool}.

_Survival_{: .glossary #survival}
: The fundamental _biological imperative_{: .cool} *driving all* [evolutionary processes]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}). In the context of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) and [evolutionary psychology]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), this *relentless drive* _shaped_{: .cool} not only our _physical adaptations_{: .bold} but also our [cognitive biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}), [in-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}) [tribalism]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }}), and cultural toolkits—optimizing the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) for _immediate gene-pool success and survival rather than for_{: .mark} [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _truth-seeking_{: .mark}.

_Survival Implications_{: .glossary #survival-implications}
: The [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) consequences a [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) or trait had on an organism's ability to live, reproduce and/or support its kin group (or otherwise help the [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) of the gene pool) in our ancestral, pre-agricultural environment. (See also: Kin Selection/Inclusive Fitness]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#inclusive-fitness-kin-selection))

_Suppression_{: .glossary #suppression}
: The **deliberate**, *systemic effort* by [institutions]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/state-institutions/" | relative_url }}) and cultural authorities to stifle both [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _truth_{: .cool} and [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) _human biology_{: .bold} in order to maintain artificial [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). On an informational level, suppression hides, discredits, or destroys [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) *when it threatens* the entrenched [power]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#power) structures, rigid [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies), or comforting [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) of the state. Crucially, this _censorship_{: .warn} directly enables a second, far more intimate form of control: _the profound physical and psychological suppression of the human animal_{: .mark}. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), _our species thrived in autonomous, highly active, multi-generational foraging bands_{: .mark}. Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), modern [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) engineered institutions—such as the isolated [nuclear family]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/family/" | relative_url }}), rigid standardized schooling, and sedentary corporate workplaces—that require the **ruthless suppression** of our [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) *evolutionary drives* for _movement_{: .cool}, *diverse* social _bonding_{: .cool}, and [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy). This creates a devastating [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). To _prevent constant rebellion against this unnatural cage_{: .mark}, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) relies heavily on informational suppression, using moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) to convince us that the crushing of our biological hardware is actually a culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), polite necessity. Any friction or distress is systematically [medicalised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#medicalisation) or **punished**, _suppressing the root cause of the suffering_{: .mark}. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By looking at both [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) and [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can demystify these [coercive]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion) tactics, _recognizing that modern institutions only survive by suppressing the evidence of their own_{: .mark} [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) _alongside the basic requirements of human flourishing_{: .mark}.

_Taboo_{: .glossary #taboo}
: A strong, [culturally constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) **prohibition** against a *specific* [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}), word, or topic. Taboos are *primarily* [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _mechanisms_{: .warn} used by societies to **enforce** [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) and *protect* [traditional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#tradition) [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies), frequently penalising behaviours that are biologically [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) but _socially inconvenient_{: .bold}.

_The Clan_{: .glossary #the-clan}
: The _foundational_{: .neon} _social unit_{: .bold} of [human evolutionary history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}). For *millions of years* (**99.9%** of our existence), humans and earlier humans survived not as _isolated_{: .warn} individuals or _nuclear_{: .warn} households, but within porous, multi-generational networks (roughly 50-150 people). _The Clan_{: .cool} provided _massive redundancy_{: .bold}, defined by *constant* physical _proximity_{: .cool}, shared _child-rearing_{: .cool} ([alloparenting]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#alloparenting)), [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) _resource sharing_{: .cool}, and *profound* _emotional_{: .cool} [co-regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation). These _biological necessities have been completely structurally dismantled by modern societies, so there is no longer collective buffering against economic or physical shocks_{: .mark}. The structural _collapse_{: .warn} of _The Clan_{: .cool} following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) _is arguably the most devastating_{: .mark} [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) _of the modern era_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [The Clan]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }})

_The Herd_{: .glossary #the-herd}
: A **polemical term** describing the _uncritical_{: .warn} mass of people who **blindly follow** [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) cultural [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), social [dogmas]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma), or [authority figures]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority). Driven by an [evolved instinct]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) for [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) and a [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of the [out-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}), [the herd]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-herd) _prioritizes social safety and tribal belonging over_{: .mark} [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _truth or independent_{: .mark} [critical thinking]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}).

**See Also:** [Thinking Guide]({{ "/guide/" | relative_url }}), a page that discusses "The Herd".

_Theory_{: .glossary #theory}
: A _comprehensive, well-substantiated explanation of some aspect of the natural world that is built upon a vast body of facts, laws, logical inferences, and_{: .mark} [tested]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) [hypotheses]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hypothesis). Unlike the casual use of the word to mean a "hunch" or "guess", a [scientific theory]({{ "/guide/first-principles/scientific-method/" | relative_url }}) represents the highest level of certainty in science because it has repeatedly withstood rigorous, independent [testing]({{ "/guide/first-principles/testability/" | relative_url }}) and scrutiny.

_Therapy Culture_{: .glossary #therapy-culture}experiment
: A modern _societal shift_{: .bold} where *everyday emotions*, [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) struggles, and [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) are [medicalised]({{ "/case-studies/pseudoscience/medicalisation/" | relative_url }}) and **"managed"** by so-called **"professionals"**. Rather than _fixing systemic issues caused by the_{: .mark} [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), it frequently focuses on enforcing [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity) and pathologising [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _human variation_{: .cool} under the _guise_{: .warn} of **"healing"**.

**See also:** [Therapy Culture]({{ "/guide/human-history/therapy-culture/" | relative_url }})

_The Two Cultures_{: .glossary #the-two-cultures}
: A dangerous _epistemological_{: .bold} overlap where _unevidenced_{: .warn} [ideologies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology) from _the humanities_{: .bold} or _social sciences_{: .bold} *adopt* the [sciency-sounding]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sciency-sounding) language of [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) or physics. This creates a powerful illusion of [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) authority, allowing the [secular priesthood]({{ "/guide/obstacles/secular-priesthood/" | relative_url }}) to enforce purely [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), untestable [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) _as if they were established scientific facts_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [The Two Cultures]({{ "/articles/science/two-cultures/" | relative_url }})

_Thinking For Yourself_{: .glossary #thinking-for-yourself}
: The **rejection** of _inherited_{: .bold} social [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and modern [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). In the ancestral environment, breaking [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) with [the tribe]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/the-clan/" | relative_url }}) often meant death. Today, [thinking for yourself]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/" | relative_url }}) requires *overriding* that **primal biological** [fear]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#fear) of _ostracism_{: .warn} in order to _evaluate_{: .cool} modern, [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) systems against [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) reality.

_Tolerance and Expectation_{: .glossary #tolerance}
: In an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) context, the *shifting* [psychological]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) _baseline_{: .bold} of *human expectation*. As scientific _innovation_{: .neon} systematically *eliminates* [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) ancestral threats (like high child mortality and extreme [poverty]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#poverty)), modern [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) _artificially shrink our tolerance for any remaining societal friction despite this progress or improvement_{: .mark}. This _manufactured_{: .warn} **intolerance** is frequently **weaponised** by the [mass media]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mass-media) for profit and by the [secular priesthood]({{ "/guide/obstacles/secular-priesthood/" | relative_url }}) to justify expanding [top-down control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }}) and [institutional perpetuation]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/institutional-control/" | relative_url }}).

_Top-Down Control_{: .glossary #top-down-control}
: A system of [governance]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/" | relative_url }}) or organization where _decisions_{: .bold}, [rules]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#rules), and commands are _issued strictly by a centralized_{: .mark} [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) _at the top and enforced upon the rest of the population below_{: .mark}.

**See also:** [Top-Down Control]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/top-down-control/" | relative_url }})

_Toxic Masculinity_{: .glossary #toxic-masculinity}
: A [culturally constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction), heavily [weaponised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [label]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#labeling). While frequently used in [modern narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to describe *genuinely harmful behaviours*, from an [evidence-based]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}) perspective, it often serves to [medicalise]({{ "/case-studies/pseudoscience/medicalisation/" | relative_url }}) or [pathologise]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#pathologize) men's predictable, **distressed reactions** to severe [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). By [blaming]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/blame/" | relative_url }}) the individual's **"masculinity"**, [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) effectively ignores the systemic failures, [isolation]({{ "/case-studies/health/fortress-of-privacy/" | relative_url }}), and [loss of purpose]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#aimlessness) that actually drive the destructive [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}).

_Tradition_{: .glossary #tradition}
: The _generational passing down_{: .mark} of _customs_{: .bold}, [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), and **societal rules** within a [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture). For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), passing down knowledge within [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands was strictly an [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) and *practical endeavor*. The tribe _retained behaviours that objectively aided_{: .mark} [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival), [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation), and _group cohesion_{: .bold}; _if a practice stopped working, the immediate environment forced them to adapt and discard it_{: .mark}. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), tradition _ceased_{: .warn} to be *a fluid [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) toolkit* and fossilized into rigid, top-down dogma. Today, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) actively [weaponises]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) tradition to protect deeply [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). By elevating entirely [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) fictions into *untouchable* sacred rites, they **demand blind** [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience) and enforce _artificial_{: .warn} [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). Phrases such as: "This is how we have always done it", "So we're all wrong, are we?" and similar thought-terminating clichés—act as a circular justification designed specifically to hide [uncomfortable]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#uncomfortable-truths) [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}), **block** _social progress_{: .bold}, **preserve** _unearned power_{: .bold}, and _deflect_{: .warn} [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }})-based _scrutiny_{: .cool}, even when those traditions maintain a severe [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By assessing cultural practices from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can strip away the unearned reverence for culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) habits, keeping only those that demonstrably serve [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _human flourishing_{: .cool} while _relentlessly discarding the oppressive ghosts of the past_{: .mark}.

_Transparency_{: .glossary}
: The structural _openness_{: .cool} and a_ccessibility_{: .cool} of data/evdience, methods, and decision-making processes within an institution, [experiment]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#experiment), or system. In both science and governance, transparency is the **enemy of unevidenced** [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority); _it allows independent observers to scrutinize, verify, and replicate processes, ensuring that claims and actions are judged openly on their merits rather than hidden behind institutional walls_{: .mark}.

_Transparency_{: .glossary #transparency}
: The structural _openness_{: .cool} and *absolute* _accessibility_{: .cool} of data, methods, and decision-making processes within an institution, system, or [experiment]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#experiment). For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), social organization and leadership were *inherently* _transparent_{: .cool}. Within [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands, close-knit living and [shared eyes]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#shared-eyes) meant that resource _distribution_{: .cool}, conflict _resolution_{: .cool}, and the [behaviour]({{ "/guide/human-history/psychology/" | relative_url }}) of leaders were *constantly observable*; _accountability was immediate, horizontal, and_{: .mark} [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), _power centralised behind the impenetrable walls of palaces, courtrooms, and boardrooms_{: .mark}. Today, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) actively [weaponises]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) opacity—using bureaucratic **jargon**, proprietary **algorithms**, and closed-door **protocols**—to shield their [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) fictions and [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) from public _scrutiny_{: .cool}. (Many secular instsitutioons from Government beuracracies, Social Services, the Probation Service, etc, enact policy that supress transparency. For example, someone "released on licence" to the supervision of the Probation service is _prohibited_{: .warn} from _recording interactions_{: .cool}. _Why?_{: .warn} _Would transparency not increase_{: .mark} [legitimacy]({{ "/case-studies/politics/governance/legitimacy/" | relative_url }})?) In both a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) and _effective governance_{: .cool}, _transparency_{: .bold} is the ultimate enemy of **unevidenced** [authority]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#authority) and [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion). _It allows independent observers to scrutinize, verify, and replicate processes, ensuring that claims are judged purely on their_{: .mark} [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _merits rather than institutional prestige_{: .mark}. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires wiping the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate) and assessing systems strictly from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}). By doing so, we must *relentlessly demand* _transparency_{: .cool}, _recognising that any authority requiring secrecy to survive is fundamentally hiding a lack of_{: .mark} [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}).

_Treating Symptoms_{: .glossary #treating-symptoms}
: The _systemic, highly profitable practice of addressing the superficial consequences of our profound_{: .mark} [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) _rather than fixing the structural root cause_{: .mark}. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), *essential* _human needs_{: .cool}—such as [alloparenting]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#alloparenting), [emotional regulation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#co-regulation), conflict _resolution_{: .cool}, and elder care—_were handled organically and_{: .mark} _for free_{: .cool} _by the horizontal, distributed architecture of_{: .mark} [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) and the _Industrial Revolution_{: .bold}, the _extended tribe_{: .cool} was **systematically dismantled** to create the isolated, atomized nuclear family. Because our _biological hardware's_{: .terminal} demand for _connection_{: .cool} and _safety_{: .cool} *did not disappear*, _a massive evolutionary vacuum was created_{: .mark}. _Capitalism_{: .bold} and the [state bureaucracy]({{ "/case-studies/politics/bureaucratic-machine/" | relative_url }}) rushed in to _commoditise_{: .warn} this void, creating a "Patchwork Economy." Today, a [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) of professionalised services—from the [Offender Treatment Industry]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#offender-treatment-industry) and the _charity complex_{: .bold} to the highly [medicalised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#medicalisation) mental health sector—acts as **expensive, artificial scaffolding** to prevent total societal collapse. These entrenched [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) merely *bandage* the **trauma of isolation** while *deliberately ignoring* the **missing pillar** of _genuine community_{: .cool}. Achieving true [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By _examining_{: .cool} **human suffering** from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can _recognise_{: .cool} that **we do not need** more culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) industries to manage our [dysfunction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction); _we need to restore the_{: .mark} [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _structural support of group living_{: .mark}.

**See Also:** [Case Study: Treating Symptoms]({{ "/case-studies/society/normal-living/treating-symptoms/" | relative_url }})

_Tribalism_{: .glossary #tribalism}
: The _hardwired_{: .terminal}, [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) _biological drive_{: .bold} to form tightly knit social groups and prioritise [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) *loyalty* and *defence*. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), _this was a strictly_{: .mark} [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _and necessary survival mechanism_{: .mark}. Within [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands, profound _loyalty_{: .bold} to [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan) ensured *mutual* _protection_{: .cool}, *shared* _resources_{: .cool}, and *collective* [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), humans were **absorbed** into massive, anonymous societies, _stripping away_{: .warn} the horizontal, face-to-face _community_{: .cool} we *evolved to need*. This created a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Because _our biological desperation to belong to a tribe remains_{: .mark}, but the *actual clan is missing*, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) actively [weaponises]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) this *ancient* _hardware_{: .terminal}. They manufacture _artificial_{: .warn}, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) tribes—political parties, nationalistic borders, religious sects, and corporate identities. By _hacking_{: .terminal} our [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) *need for connection*, they _compel_{: .warn} individuals to fiercely defend the group's culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) and [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}), even when those fictions **violently contradict** [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) truth and [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining our own [psychological biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }}) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), _we can recognise when our ancient loyalty is being exploited_{: .mark} for modern [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion), _allowing us_{: .cool} to *prioritise empirical reality* over _blind_{: .warn} **allegiance to a manufactured tribe**.

**See also:** [Tribalism]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-dynamics/tribalism/" | relative_url }})

_Uncomfortable Truths_{: .glossary #uncomfortable-truths}
: [Objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), [verifiable facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}) about reality, [human nature]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-nature), or [societal systems]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-ideas/" | relative_url }}) that directly clash with popular cultural narratives, [comforting myths]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), or deeply held [personal beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs). Because the [human brain]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/brain/" | relative_url }}) instinctively craves reassurance and social [conformity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#conformity), these [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) realities are frequently denied, ignored, or [suppressed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression) because accepting them requires rewriting an established [worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#worldview).

_Uniformity_{: .glossary #uniformity}
: The _unnatural_{: .warn} state of _sameness_{: .bold} _demanded_{: .warn} by **industrial**, [educational]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/education/" | relative_url }}), and [political systems]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-ideas/" | relative_url }}). It actively _conflicts_{: .warn} with the [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _biological variation_{: .cool} and _neurodiversity_{: .cool} *inherent* in [human nature]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-nature).

_Unstructured Play_{: .glossary #unstructured-play}
: The primary, _hardwired_{: .terminal} _biological mechanism_{: .cool} through which [mammalian]({{ "/case-studies/who-we-are/mammalian-care-blueprint/" | relative_url }}) brains *acquire* complex social, cognitive, and physical _skills_{: .cool}. In post-Agricultural societies, this vital [evolutionary]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}) _necessity_{: .bold} is frequently [suppressed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression), marginalised, or _replaced_{: .warn} by rigid, adult-directed [standardisation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#standardisation), fundamentally starving [children]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#role-of-children) of their [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) *developmental environment*.

**See Also:** [Play]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/play/" | relative_url }})

_Urbanisation_{: .glossary #urbanisation}
: _The massive, ongoing historical migration of human populations from rural, open environments into dense, highly artificial cities and towns_{: .mark}.

_Value-Based Claim_{: .glossary #value-based-claim}
: A _subjective_{: .bold} or [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) statement regarding what a [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) or individual believes **"ought"** to be done, _based on moral, cultural, or emotional preferences rather than strictly measurable data_{: .mark}. While essential for organizing human communities, these claims *must not be confused* with [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}), as they frequently reflect the _artificial_{: .warn}, _constructed_{: .bold} [norms]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/norms/" | relative_url }}) and historical [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) of a specific [culture]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#culture).

_Values_{: .glossary #values}
: [Culturally constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction), [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) *directives* and [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) concerning what a [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) deems important, moral, or acceptable. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), the _"values"_{: .bold} of [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands _were not abstract philosophical debates; they were strictly_{: .mark} [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) _survival mechanics_{: .mark}. _Behaviours_{: .bold} like [reciprocity]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#reciprocity), resource sharing, and the active suppression of dominance were *organically* enforced because they objectively kept the [in-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group) alive. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), human communities scaled into massive, stratified societies, and _the function of values radically changed_{: .mark}. **Abstract** moral codes were _engineered_{: .warn} by "elites", such as Royalty and Religious and [Secular Priesthoods]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood), to protect hoarded wealth and enforce rigid [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). By employing heavy moral [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing), authorities successfully _disguised_{: .warn} **highly artificial fictions**—such as blind [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience), unquestioning patriotism, or the criminalization of [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) [sexuality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sexuality)—as "undeniable", objective truths. (For one example, see [The Great Chain of Being]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/class-control/" | relative_url }})). Because these **artificial values** frequently *contradict* our _biological hardware_{: .terminal}, they create severe [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}), serving as oppressive [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}) that *prioritise* **institutional stability** over human [well-being]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#wellbeing). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires applying a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining [morality]({{ "/case-studies/morality/" | relative_url }}) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can *strip away* the **unearned reverence** for culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _values_{: .bold} that exist only to enforce [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion), returning our focus to the [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }})-based requirements of genuine [human flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing).

**See Also:** [Case Study: Values]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/values/" | relative_url }})

_Vote vs. Fact_{: .glossary #vote-vs-fact}
: A **fundamental structural** _flaw_{: .warn} in modern [democratic]({{ "/case-studies/politics/democracy/" | relative_url }}) systems where a culturally engineered [consensus]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#consensus) or *majority vote of a population* is permitted to legally override [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}), scientifically verifiable reality. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), decision-making within [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands was strictly bound by [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). The tribe **could not** simply _"vote"_{: .bold} that a poisonous plant was edible, or that a predator was harmless; _physical reality aggressively enforced itself_{: .mark}, and [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) was anchored in [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }}). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the *creation* of massive, abstract societies allowed [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) **fictions** to *take precedence* over [biology]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#biology) and physics. Today, a [democratic]({{ "/case-studies/politics/democracy/" | relative_url }}) majority can legally mandate that an [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}) is actually "a personal moral failure", or legislate entirely against environmental reality. The [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) actively _exploits_{: .warn} this vulnerability, utilizing mass media [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and emotional [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}) to manufacture a popular vote that protects their unearned [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) and [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining [political systems]({{ "/case-studies/politics/political-ideas/" | relative_url }}) from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we must *confront a stark reality*: _truth is not_{: .mark} [democratic]({{ "/case-studies/politics/democracy/" | relative_url }}). While _voting_{: .bold} is a necessary [harm reduction]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#harm-reduction) tool for *navigating subjective preferences*, no amount of culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) consensus can ever alter a [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), objective [fact]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }}).

**See Also:** [Boundary of the Ballot]({{ "/case-studies/politics/democracy/boundary-of-the-ballot/" | relative_url }})

_Weaponise_{: .glossary #weaponise}
: The **deliberate** _hijacking_{: .warn} and *redirection* of a _tool_{: .bold}, _concept_{: .bold}, or [innate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#innate) biological _trait_{: .bold} for a hostile, controlling, or coercive purpose. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), our _hardwired_{: .terminal} cognitive [biases]({{ "/guide/thinking-for-yourself/cognitive-biases/" | relative_url }})—such as a _hyper-vigilance_{: .cool} to danger, *wariness of the unknown*, or a desperate need for _social approval_{: .cool}—were strictly [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival) mechanisms that safely guided [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging bands. However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the creation of massive, anonymous societies caused a *profound* [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Because our ancient hardware remains **highly reactive**, the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) _actively weaponises these vulnerabilities to maintain their unearned_{: .mark} [power]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#power). Through mass media [framing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) and manufactured [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), they constantly trigger our biological reflexes to enforce top-down [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma). They _weaponise_{: .warn} our [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) need for _belonging_{: .cool} into **rabid nationalism**, our _caution_{: .cool} into **violent fear** of the [out-group]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#in-group-and-out-group), and our _negativity bias_{: .bold} into unquestioning [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience) to [dysfunctional]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dysfunction) [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By understanding human neurobiology from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can recognise when our own [evolved]({{ "/guide/human-history/evolution/" | relative_url }}) instincts are being actively _hacked_{: .terminal} for institutional [coercion]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#coercion), _allowing us to reclaim our_{: .mark} [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy) _and reject the manipulation_{: .mark}.

_Wellbeing_{: .glossary #wellbeing}
: An [objective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) biological state of physical and psychological [flourishing]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-flourishing). For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), true wellbeing was not a luxury, an individual pursuit, or a _commodified_{: .warn} industry; it was the _default,_{: .bold} [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical) byproduct of *living within* an [egalitarian]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#egalitarian) foraging band. The _active fulfillment of our ancient neurobiological needs—daily movement, profound social connection within_{: .mark} [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan), [unstructured play]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/play/" | relative_url }}), [autonomy]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#autonomy), and a fluid, shame-free [sexuality]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#sexuality)—_was organically woven into the fabric of daily_{: .mark} [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), these foundational pillars were *systematically* **stripped away** to serve the mechanical demands of mass civilization and rigid [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies). Today, we suffer a devastating [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}), trapped within an [architecture of isolation]({{ "/case-studies/health/fortress-of-privacy/" | relative_url }}) that _starves_{: .warn} our _biological hardware_{: .terminal}. To maintain the [status quo]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#status-quo), the [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) [frames]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#framing) culturally [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) **compliance** and *economic productivity* as _"success"_{: .bold}, whilst deeply [medicalising]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#medicalisation) the *inevitable* _distress_{: .warn} caused by this **starved state**. They offer highly [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) band-aids—such as the commercial wellness industry or chemical [suppression]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#suppression)—_rather than fixing the structural void_{: .mark}. Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires a [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to wipe the [slate clean]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By _assessing_{: .cool} *human needs* from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we _realise_{: .neon} that *genuine wellbeing* cannot be achieved merely through the absence of disease or [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience) to societal [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}); it demands the active, [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _nourishment of the human animal_{: .mark}.

_Witch Hunts/Witch Trials_{: .glossary #witch-trials}
: Historical periods of [moral panic]({{ "/case-studies/politics/dysfunction/moral-panics/" | relative_url }}) where [religious]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#religion) [doctrine]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#doctrine) and legal systems **colluded** to harass, _persecute_{: .warn} _or even execute individuals based entirely on supernatural_{: .mark} [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs) _and unevidenced accusations_{: .mark}. Crucially, the *underlying* _psychological mindset_{: .bold}—the tribal urge to [scapegoat]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scapegoats), purge, and brutally punish an [out-group]({{ "/case-studies/us-vs-them/" | relative_url }}) based on _hysteria_{: .warn} rather than [facts]({{ "/guide/first-principles/correct-answers/" | relative_url }})—_remains deeply embedded in_{: .mark} [human nature]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#human-nature). If we fail to *genuinely learn* from these past _horrors_{: .warn} and _ignore this exact mindset germinating in modern_{: .mark} [ideologies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#ideology), we **risk** repeating *the same cycles* of [mistreatment]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#mistreatment), persecution, imprisonment, and state-sanctioned violence, torture and/or execution *under new names*.

_Work and Productivity_{: .glossary #work-and-productivity}
: In our [evolutionary history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), work was the [natural]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}), _collaborative_{: .cool}, and *varied effort* required for immediate tribal [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival). Following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}) and industrialisation, it was _transformed_{: .bold} into a [socially constructed]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#social-construction) system of specialised, _repetitive_{: .warn} labour designed to extract surplus value and serve abstract institutional metrics, frequently resulting in a profound [evolutionary mismatch]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}).

**See also:** [Work and Productivity]({{ "/guide/human-history/work-and-productivity/" | relative_url }})

_Worldview_{: .glossary #worldview}
: The comprehensive _framework_{: .bold} of [beliefs]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#beliefs), [values]({{ "/case-studies/society/sociology/values/" | relative_url }}), [narratives]({{ "/case-studies/narratives/" | relative_url }}), and **assumptions** through which an individual or [society]({{ "/case-studies/society/social-structures/" | relative_url }}) _interprets reality, processes information, and makes moral or practical decisions_{: .mark}. For **over 99.5%** of [human history]({{ "/guide/human-history/" | relative_url }}), the human _operating system_{: .terminal} was largely unified: an [empirical]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#empirical), [survival]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#survival)-driven lens embedded in the _horizontal_{: .cool} [cooperation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#cooperation) of [the clan]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#the-clan). However, following the [Agricultural Transition]({{ "/guide/human-history/agricultural-transition/" | relative_url }}), the need to coordinate massive, anonymous populations triggered an explosion of [historical and alternative worldviews]({{ "/articles/philosophy/worldviews/" | relative_url }}). From the strict [hierarchies]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#hierarchies) of Confucianism and Legalism in China, to the karmic liberation of Hinduism and Buddhism in India, to the rational resilience of Stoicism in the Greco-Roman world, these *diverse systems* functioned as highly sophisticated, [intersubjective]({{ "/guide/first-principles/intersubjective/" | relative_url }}) _software_{: .terminal}. _While some offered profound psychological utility, many were rapidly_{: .mark} [weaponised]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#weaponise) _by rulers, religious priesthoods and early iterations of the_{: .mark} [secular priesthood]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#secular-priesthood) to _enforce_{: .warn} **artificial** [dogma]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#dogma), _demand_{: .warn} **blind** [obedience]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#obedience), and _justify_{: .warn} **deep** [evolutionary mismatches]({{ "/case-studies/mismatches/" | relative_url }}). Achieving intellectual [emancipation]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#emancipation) requires wiping the [clean slate]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#clean-slate). By examining these myriad philosophies from [first principles]({{ "/guide/first-principles/" | relative_url }}), we can *recognise* them not as "undeniable universal truths", but as **man-made coping mechanisms** and societal [lock-ins]({{ "/case-studies/locked-in/" | relative_url }}). This _historical plethora of manufactured realities_{: .mark} *underscores* _exactly why_{: .cool} humanity ultimately required a strictly [evidence]({{ "/guide/first-principles/evidence/" | relative_url }})-based [scientific worldview]({{ "/glossary/" | relative_url }}#scientific-worldview) to _finally separate objective fact from culturally_{: .mark} [normal]({{ "/guide/human-history/natural-vs-normal/" | relative_url }}) _fiction_{: .mark}.
